        ### Task Summary

        ### Constraints
        - MUST NOT modify the orchestrator's existing worker_startup.template.sh, render_startup_script, or any production lifecycle code path. Harness is additive — new files under reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/.
- MUST NOT interfere with production orchestrator runs. Spoofed tasks MUST be scoped to the test user ([REDACTED]). Stage 1 must confirm how task routing works — if pool-wide, use a dedicated live-test project and a params.live_test=true marker so a production worker claiming a test task is harmless.
- MUST NOT force-push or push to main. Variant B pushes to temp branch live-test/<timestamp>-<short-hash> and deletes on success. Never --no-verify, never amend shared commits.
- MUST NOT clobber pods doing real user work. Variant B safety gate is mandatory: reject takeover if pod has any tasks In Progress for worker_id, or fresh (<60s) heartbeat.
- MUST include an opt-out flag (env REIGH_LIVE_TEST_NO_TERMINATE=1 and/or --no-terminate CLI flag) that skips terminate_pod() on both normal-exit and exception paths.
- MUST resolve token->user via the CURRENT schema (plain `token` column). If stage 1 discovers production still uses jti_hash, fall back to SHA256(token) hex. Do not guess — verify first.
- MUST use exact task_status enum capitalization: 'Queued', 'In Progress', 'Complete', 'Failed', 'Cancelled'.
- MUST use z_image_turbo_i2i (with _i2i suffix) for the I2I matrix entry, not z_image_turbo.
- Task matrix failures reported per-task (8 rows), not all-or-nothing. 7/8 passing still produces a useful report.
- Per-task completion polling must have a configurable timeout (suggest 20 min default) and capture final status + tasks.output_location + generations.id list within project scope + time window.
- Variant B post-run cleanup: restore pod to previous branch/commit via git checkout <prev>, THEN delete temp remote branch. On failure, preserve temp branch and print its name.
- Secrets (RUNPOD_API_KEY, SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY, SSH private key) loaded from existing .env locations (reigh-worker-orchestrator/.env) — never hardcoded or prompted for.

        ### Suggested Approach
        Stage 1 (prep/finalize unknowns): (a) Verify user_api_tokens schema state in production by reading migrations in order and running a read-only schema probe via DatabaseClient — confirm plain token column vs jti_hash. (b) Confirm how individual-user worker routing scopes task claims — is there a user/worker identity filter in claim-next-task, or is the queue pool-wide? If pool-wide, create a dedicated 'live-test' project under the target user and tag tasks with params.live_test=true so any stray claim by a real worker is harmless. (c) Skim .megaplan/plans/build-a-spoofable-end-to-end-20260410-1456/ and look-at-how-we-create-tasks-20260410-1842/ for reusable primitives. (d) Confirm RunPod GPU type + template_id (read orchestrator config defaults in config.py). (e) Pin the 8 payload shapes by reading artifacts/worker-matrix/* + qwen_image_style/qwen_image_2512/qwen_image_edit handler param expectations. Deliverables: stage1_findings.md. Files touched: none (read-only). Risks: production schema may differ from checked-in migrations. Validation: schema probe returns expected columns; routing confirmed from claim-next-task edge source.

Stage 2 (shared primitives): Create reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/ package: (i) token_resolver.py — token->user_id using confirmed schema. (ii) project_for_user.py — get-or-create a dedicated live-test project. (iii) task_spoofer.py — insert_task(task_type, params, project_id) loading reference payloads from artifacts/worker-matrix/ and mutating per test. (iv) completion_poller.py — poll_until_complete(task_id, timeout_sec) returns (final_status, output_location, generation_ids) via tasks + generations tables. (v) ssh_bootstrap.py — wraps SSHClient with clone_repo_into(dir, url, branch), run_uv_sync(workdir), kill_supervisor_and_worker(), launch_worker(workdir, cli_args, worker_id, env). (vi) heartbeat_waiter.py — poll workers table until fresh heartbeat + phase=ready. (vii) terminate_guard.py — honors env/CLI opt-out. Deliverables: 7 primitive modules + unit tests with mocked DatabaseClient/SSHClient. Files touched: new under scripts/live_test/. Risks: drift from orchestrator helper semantics. Validation: unit tests pass; ruff/mypy clean.

Stage 3 (Variant A driver — fresh install): variant_a.py calls create_pod_and_wait (reusing orchestrator GPU config + template + env_vars, injecting REIGH_ACCESS_TOKEN env var), waits for SSH, runs apt deps + uv installer + `git clone https://github.com/banodoco/Reigh-Worker.git /workspace/Reigh-Worker-LiveTest`, cd, `uv sync --locked --extra cuda124`, then launches worker via `nohup uv run --python 3.10 --extra cuda124 python worker.py --supabase-url <url> [REDACTED] --worker <pod_id> --wgp-profile 3 --idle-release-minutes 0 > logs/startup.log 2>&1 &`. Calls heartbeat_waiter, then runs the 8-task matrix via task_spoofer + completion_poller, aggregates results, prints report, calls terminate_guard. Deliverables: variant_a.py + CLI entry. Risks: RunPod timeouts; apt mirror issues; uv network hiccups. Validation: dry-run passes; first real run with --no-terminate succeeds on at least qwen_image (fast task).

Stage 4 (Variant B driver — update existing): variant_b.py takes --pod-id (existing) or spawns + does standard install first. Runs safety_gate. Commits local reigh-worker tree: `git -C reigh-worker checkout -b live-test/<ts>-<sha>`, `git add`, `git commit`, `git push -u origin <branch>`. SSH in, `cd /workspace/Reigh-Worker && git fetch origin && PREV_REF=$(git rev-parse HEAD) && git checkout <branch> && git pull && uv sync --locked --extra cuda124`. kill_supervisor_and_worker, re-launch with --reigh-access-token. heartbeat_waiter. Same 8-task matrix + reporting. On cleanup: SSH `git checkout $PREV_REF`, delete temp remote branch. On failure: preserve temp branch, print name. Deliverables: variant_b.py. Risks: submodule git state; credentials; supervisor races; clobbering real work. Validation: safety gate unit test; first real run on freshly-spawned test pod, not a production pod.

Stage 5 (dry-run/smoke-test harness itself): Before touching a real GPU: unit-test pass on primitives with mocked DatabaseClient + SSHClient. Add --dry-run flag to both variants that (a) resolves token, (b) runs Variant B safety gate against a real pod, (c) loads every payload fixture and validates shape against handler params, (d) does NOT spawn pods or push branches. Catches schema drift and missing secrets without burning GPU minutes. Deliverables: --dry-run on both drivers + scripts/live_test/smoke.py. Validation: smoke prints expected plan for both variants.

Stage 6 (first real run): Run Variant A first (lower blast radius). Use --no-terminate on first attempt so failed pods stay up for SSH debugging. Iterate until all 8 tasks pass, then promote to terminate-on-success. Then Variant B against a freshly-spawned-and-standard-installed pod (not a production pod). Only use reused persistent-volume pods once the driver is proven. Deliverables: two successful run reports (JSON + log) in .megaplan/plans/<slug>/runs/. Risks: real worker bugs surfaced (good!), test infra bugs (iterate). Validation: report shows 8/8 pass.

Stage 7 (cleanup/documentation): Purge lingering temp branches on banodoco/Reigh-Worker (`git ls-remote | grep live-test/` should be empty). Update reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/README or a new docs/live_test.md ONLY if user requests docs — per project policy, do not proactively create docs. Add .gitignore entry for report output directory. NEVER check in the test token — load from env or untracked local file. Validation: stale branches purged; .gitignore updated.

File layout: reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/ (alongside existing create_test_task.py, test_supabase.py) — naturally shares DatabaseClient + runpod primitives without cross-repo imports. Keep reigh-worker itself untouched — no worker-side changes needed.

Top risks: (1) Task routing: if tasks aren't actually scoped per-user, test tasks could be stolen by real workers or vice versa — stage 1 MUST resolve this before stage 2 work starts. (2) --idle-release-minutes 0 may cause the worker to exit before claiming the first task if queue polling is slow — consider 1 minute with a bumped idle_onboarding_grace_seconds. (3) Supervisor restart: killing worker.py alone causes relaunch with OLD args — must kill supervisor first. (4) Variant B git push requires credentials on the dev machine. (5) terminate_pod has no real opt-out in orchestrator code, so the flag must be enforced in the harness's own code path — honor it on both normal-exit AND exception paths.

        The engineering brief above was produced by analyzing the codebase. Use it as a strong starting point, but the suggested approach is a hypothesis — verify it's the best fix, not just a valid one.

        Idea:
Design a robust end-to-end live test harness for the Reigh platform that exercises the individual-user worker path (NOT the orchestrator-managed worker path) against a specific user identified by Reigh access [REDACTED] Two variants of one live test, both targeting a real RunPod GPU, both creating real spoof tasks in Supabase for that specific user, both waiting for actual task completion and verifying generations were produced end-to-end. Variant A 'Fresh Install': spin up a new RunPod pod using the orchestrator's create_pod_and_wait approach, SSH in and clone reigh-worker into a folder with a NEW name (NOT Reigh-Worker, e.g. Reigh-Worker-LiveTest), run buildWorkerLaunchLine-equivalent install/launch with [REDACTED] --wgp-profile 3 --idle-release-minutes 0, wait for heartbeat, insert spoof tasks in Supabase scoped to the user that the token resolves to (via user_api_tokens.jti_hash), poll until each task reaches Complete with generations row, report pass/fail per task with output URLs, terminate pod via terminate_pod() with opt-out flag. Variant B 'Update Existing': target an existing pod with /workspace/Reigh-Worker/ installed (or spawn+standard-install then take over), push current local working copy of reigh-worker/ to a temporary branch on banodoco/Reigh-Worker, SSH in, git fetch+checkout+pull, uv sync --locked, cleanly restart worker with --reigh-access-token for test user, run same task matrix, on success optionally delete temp branch and restore pod to previous branch/state. Variant B must not clobber pods doing real user work — include safety check for in-flight tasks. Task matrix (one of each): travel_orchestrator standard Wan 2 images 1 segment, travel_orchestrator LTX variant, individual_travel_segment single segment, qwen_image_style with style ref, qwen_image text-to-image, qwen_image_2512, qwen_image_edit, z_image_turbo I2I. Stages: (1) prep/finalize unknowns, (2) build shared primitives (token→user resolver, task spoofer, poll-until-complete, SSH helpers reused from orchestrator), (3) build Variant A driver, (4) build Variant B driver, (5) dry-run/smoke-test the harness itself, (6) first real run, (7) cleanup/documentation. For each stage: concrete deliverables, files to touch, risks, validation.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Live E2E Test Harness for Individual-User Worker Path

## Overview

Build a live, additive test harness under `reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/` that exercises the individual-user worker code path against a real RunPod GPU and real Supabase, scoped to the user owning Reigh access token `3HKcoLeJAFFfTFFeRV6Eu7Lq`. Two drivers share the same primitives and 8-task matrix:

- **Variant A — Fresh Install:** `create_pod_and_wait` → SSH clone `banodoco/Reigh-Worker` into `/workspace/Reigh-Worker-LiveTest` → `uv sync` → launch worker with `--reigh-access-token` → run matrix → terminate (unless opt-out).
- **Variant B — Update Existing:** safety-gate an existing pod (or freshly spawned one with standard install), push local `reigh-worker/` submodule to `live-test/<ts>-<sha>` temp branch, SSH `git fetch/checkout/pull`, `uv sync`, kill supervisor+worker, relaunch with `--reigh-access-token`, run matrix, restore prior ref + delete temp branch on success.

**Key confirmed facts (from code reads):**
1. `user_api_tokens` uses **plain `token` column** (migration `20250713000008_fix_user_api_tokens_structure.sql` drops `jti_hash`). Resolver is `select user_id from user_api_tokens where token = ?`.
2. Worker CLI accepts `--reigh-access-token`, `--wgp-profile`, `--idle-release-minutes` (via `idle_release.add_cli_args`) — verified at `reigh-worker/source/runtime/worker/server.py:226-229,234`.
3. `claim-next-task` has a **PAT path** that scopes claims by `p_user_id: callerId` (`reigh-app/supabase/functions/claim-next-task/index.ts:173-208`). When the worker authenticates with `--reigh-access-token`, claims are naturally scoped to the token owner. This resolves the brief's routing concern: per-user scoping exists at the edge function level, so test tasks for the target user cannot be stolen by service-role workers running on other pools. Still use a dedicated `live-test` project + `params.live_test=true` marker for belt-and-braces isolation.
4. `terminate_pod(pod_id, api_key)` has no opt-out (`reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/runpod/api.py:223-229`); harness must guard the caller.
5. Orchestrator's `render_startup_script`/template hardcodes `Reigh-Worker` dirname and `--supabase-access-token` — **do not reuse**; harness builds its own launch command via `SSHClient.execute_command` following the brief.
6. Supervisor auto-restarts worker with old args — Variant B must kill supervisor **and** worker, then launch fresh.
7. Fixtures exist at `reigh-worker/artifacts/worker-matrix/20260316T135006000511Z/` (qwen_image_basic, z_image_turbo_basic, z_image_turbo_i2i_basic, qwen_image_edit_basic) and `.../20260316T140127343314Z/wan22_i2v_individual_segment/` — clone + mutate rather than hand-author.

**Constraints:** additive only; no edits to orchestrator production code paths or the `reigh-worker` submodule source; no force-push; no push to `main`; never hardcode the token.

---

## Phase 1: Prep / Finalize Unknowns (Read-Only)

### Step 1: Verify token schema state in production (`reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/stage1_probe.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** a short probe script that uses `DatabaseClient().supabase.table('user_api_tokens').select('id,user_id').eq('token', '<token-from-env>').limit(1).execute()` and also queries `information_schema.columns` for `user_api_tokens` to confirm `jti_hash` is absent. Token loaded from env `REIGH_LIVE_TEST_TOKEN`, never hardcoded.
2. **Run** the probe; assert (a) schema has no `jti_hash`, (b) token resolves to exactly one `user_id`. Record the resolved `user_id` for later steps.
3. **Document** findings in `scripts/live_test/stage1_findings.md` (gitignored) — schema state, resolved user_id (if sharable), RunPod defaults pulled from orchestrator config.

### Step 2: Pin runtime constants (`reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/config.py`, `worker_startup.template.sh`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Read** `gpu_orchestrator/config.py` for the default `gpu_type_id`, `image_name`, `template_id`, `network_volume_id` used by `create_pod_and_wait`. Mirror these defaults in harness config so fresh pods match production-shape.
2. **Read** `worker_startup.template.sh` around lines 167-183 and 355-366 for the install sequence (apt deps, uv install, `uv sync --locked --extra cuda124`) to replicate in Variant A's custom bootstrap.

### Step 3: Pin fixture payloads (`reigh-worker/artifacts/worker-matrix/*/captured_generation_task.json`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Read** `captured_generation_task.json` for each of: `qwen_image_basic`, `qwen_image_edit_basic`, `z_image_turbo_basic`, `z_image_turbo_i2i_basic`, `wan22_i2v_individual_segment`. Note the `params` shape and any referenced asset URLs (style refs, input images).
2. **Identify** which task types need hand-authored params (no fixture exists): `qwen_image_style`, `qwen_image_2512`, `travel_orchestrator` (Wan2 1-seg), `travel_orchestrator` (LTX variant). For each, inspect the corresponding task handler under `reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/` to confirm required `params` keys.
3. **Verify** `WGP_TASK_TYPES` at `reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/task_types.py:26-46` includes `z_image_turbo_i2i` (with suffix). Confirm `travel_orchestrator` / `individual_travel_segment` are routed separately.

**Deliverables:** `stage1_findings.md` (untracked), confirmed user_id, pod config defaults, 8 payload templates noted.
**Validation:** Schema probe returns plain token column + single user_id; fixtures load and parse as JSON.

---

## Phase 2: Shared Primitives

### Step 4: Package scaffold (`reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/__init__.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/` with `__init__.py`, `config.py` (loads token from env, loads RunPod/Supabase config from existing `.env`), and a shared `logger.py` mirroring `create_test_task.py`'s pattern. No new dependencies — use existing `DatabaseClient`, `SSHClient`, `runpod.api`.

### Step 5: Token → user resolver (`reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/token_resolver.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Implement** `resolve_token_to_user_id(db: DatabaseClient, token: str) -> str` using `db.supabase.table('user_api_tokens').select('user_id').eq('token', token).single().execute()`. Raise on zero rows or multiple rows.

### Step 6: Project + task spoofer (`reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/project_for_user.py`, `task_spoofer.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** `get_or_create_live_test_project(db, user_id) -> project_id`: query `projects` for `user_id=? and name='live-test'`; insert if missing with minimal required columns per `20250100000000_create_base_schema.sql`.
2. **Implement** `load_fixture(task_type) -> dict` that reads `reigh-worker/artifacts/worker-matrix/<run>/<case>/captured_generation_task.json` per the map pinned in Step 3. Missing types (`qwen_image_style`, `qwen_image_2512`, `travel_orchestrator` variants) get inline templates.
3. **Implement** `insert_spoof_task(db, project_id, task_type, params_overrides=None) -> task_id` that clones the fixture, merges overrides, sets `project_id`, `status='Queued'`, `params.live_test=True`, and inserts via `db.supabase.table('tasks').insert(...)` mirroring `create_test_task.py:42-58`. Use exact status capitalization `'Queued'`.

### Step 7: Completion poller (`reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/completion_poller.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** `poll_until_complete(db, task_id, project_id, timeout_sec=1200, interval_sec=5)` that polls `tasks` by id for `status in ('Complete','Failed','Cancelled')`, recording `output_location` and the `created_at` of the task on first sighting.
2. **Implement** `fetch_generations(db, project_id, since_ts, until_ts) -> list[dict]` that selects `generations` rows within the time window scoped by `project_id` (schema has no direct `task_id` FK per brief).
3. **Return** a structured `TaskResult(task_id, task_type, final_status, output_location, generation_ids, elapsed_sec, log_excerpt)`.

### Step 8: SSH bootstrap helpers (`reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/ssh_bootstrap.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Thin wrapper** around `gpu_orchestrator.runpod.ssh.SSHClient` + `get_pod_ssh_details` providing:
   - `clone_repo_into(ssh, workdir, repo_url, branch='main')` — runs `git clone --branch <branch> <url> <workdir>`.
   - `run_install(ssh, workdir)` — apt deps (mirrored from template.sh) + `cd <workdir> && uv sync --locked --extra cuda124`.
   - `kill_supervisor_and_worker(ssh)` — `pkill -f supervisor.py; pkill -f worker.py; sleep 2` with verification via `pgrep`.
   - `launch_worker(ssh, workdir, cli_args: list[str], logfile='logs/startup.log')` — builds `nohup uv run --python 3.10 --extra cuda124 python worker.py <args> > <logfile> 2>&1 &`.
   - `fetch_worker_logs(ssh, workdir, lines=200)` — `tail -n <lines>`; used for failure diagnostics.
2. **Note:** Wraps `execute_command`; does not modify `ssh.py` or `client.py`.

### Step 9: Heartbeat waiter (`reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/heartbeat_waiter.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Implement** `wait_until_ready(db, worker_id, timeout_sec=600, interval_sec=10)` that polls `workers` table by `worker_id`, returning when `last_heartbeat` > `now - 30s` AND `metadata->>startup_phase = 'ready'` AND `metadata->>ready_for_tasks = 'true'`. Mirrors semantics from `gpu_orchestrator/worker_state.py:derive_worker_state`.

### Step 10: Variant B safety gate (`reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/safety_gate.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Implement** `assert_safe_to_take_over(db, worker_id)` that raises `UnsafeTakeoverError` if:
   - `db.get_running_tasks_for_worker(worker_id)` returns any row (reuses existing helper at `gpu_orchestrator/database.py:346-366`).
   - OR `workers.last_heartbeat` is newer than 60s ago.

### Step 11: Terminate guard (`reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/terminate_guard.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Implement** `guarded_terminate(pod_id, api_key, *, no_terminate: bool)` — if `no_terminate` or env `REIGH_LIVE_TEST_NO_TERMINATE` is set to `1`, log the skip and return; else call `runpod.api.terminate_pod`.
2. **Call sites must** wrap driver try/finally so exception paths also honor the opt-out.

### Step 12: Unit tests (`reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/tests/test_primitives.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Write** pytest tests with mocked `DatabaseClient` / `SSHClient`:
   - `test_token_resolver_returns_user_id`
   - `test_token_resolver_raises_on_missing`
   - `test_task_spoofer_clones_fixture_and_sets_live_test_marker`
   - `test_completion_poller_returns_on_complete`
   - `test_completion_poller_times_out`
   - `test_safety_gate_rejects_in_progress`
   - `test_safety_gate_rejects_fresh_heartbeat`
   - `test_terminate_guard_respects_env_var`
   - `test_terminate_guard_respects_cli_flag`
   - `test_terminate_guard_skips_on_exception_path`
2. **Do not** import or mutate existing orchestrator test modules — new tests live only under `scripts/live_test/tests/`.

**Deliverables:** 7 primitive modules + `tests/test_primitives.py`.
**Validation:** `uv run pytest reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/tests/` green; existing orchestrator tests untouched and still pass.

## Phase 3: Variant A Driver (Fresh Install)

### Step 13: Driver skeleton (`reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/variant_a.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **CLI** via `argparse`: `--no-terminate`, `--dry-run`, `--wgp-profile 3`, `--idle-release-minutes 1` (default 1, not 0 — see Assumptions), `--workdir-name Reigh-Worker-LiveTest`, `--timeout-sec 1200`.
2. **Flow:**
   - Load token + secrets from env.
   - `db = DatabaseClient()`; resolve `user_id`; get-or-create project.
   - `create_pod_and_wait(...)` with RunPod defaults from Step 2; inject `env_vars={'REIGH_ACCESS_TOKEN': token}`.
   - Poll `get_pod_ssh_details` until SSH is up (reuse orchestrator's wait pattern).
   - `ssh = SSHClient(...); ssh.connect()`.
   - `clone_repo_into(ssh, '/workspace/Reigh-Worker-LiveTest', 'https://github.com/banodoco/Reigh-Worker.git', 'main')`.
   - `run_install(ssh, '/workspace/Reigh-Worker-LiveTest')`.
   - `launch_worker(ssh, '/workspace/Reigh-Worker-LiveTest', ['--supabase-url', SUPABASE_URL, '--reigh-access-token', token, '--worker', pod_id, '--wgp-profile', '3', '--idle-release-minutes', '1', '--save-logging', 'logs/worker.log'])`.
   - `wait_until_ready(db, worker_id=pod_id)`.
   - Run the 8-task matrix (Phase 5).
   - Aggregate `TaskResult` list into a report (JSON + markdown) written to `reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/runs/<ts>/variant_a.{json,md}`.
3. **Finally block:** always calls `guarded_terminate(pod_id, api_key, no_terminate=args.no_terminate)`; on exception also fetches `tail` of worker log via `fetch_worker_logs` for the report.

### Step 14: Dry-run path (`variant_a.py` dry-run branch)
**Scope:** Small
1. **When** `--dry-run`: resolve token, load all 8 fixtures, print the planned launch command + task list, exit 0 without touching RunPod or inserting tasks.

**Deliverables:** `variant_a.py` + report template.
**Validation:** `--dry-run` prints complete plan; unit tests cover the report aggregation helper.

---

## Phase 4: Variant B Driver (Update Existing)

### Step 15: Git ops helper (`reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/git_ops.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** `push_working_copy_to_temp_branch(submodule_path='reigh-worker') -> (branch_name, commit_sha, prev_ref)`:
   - `prev_ref = git -C <submodule> rev-parse HEAD`.
   - `branch = f"live-test/{ts}-{short_sha}"`.
   - `git -C <submodule> checkout -b <branch>`.
   - `git -C <submodule> add -A` + `git -C <submodule> commit -m "live-test: <ts>"` (fall through if nothing to commit — then branch == prev_ref).
   - `git -C <submodule> push -u origin <branch>` (no force, never touches main).
2. **Implement** `cleanup_temp_branch(submodule_path, branch, *, preserve: bool)`:
   - If `preserve`: log the branch name and return.
   - Else: `git -C <submodule> push origin --delete <branch>` and local `git -C <submodule> branch -D <branch>`.
3. **Implement** `restore_local_ref(submodule_path, prev_ref)` to leave the dev machine's submodule where we found it.

### Step 16: Driver (`reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/variant_b.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **CLI:** `--pod-id <id>` (required unless `--spawn-new`), `--spawn-new` (first does Variant A's standard install path via shared helper, then takes over), `--no-terminate`, `--dry-run`, `--preserve-branch-on-failure` (default true), plus the shared matrix flags.
2. **Flow:**
   - Resolve token + project.
   - `assert_safe_to_take_over(db, pod_id)`.
   - `branch, sha, prev_ref = push_working_copy_to_temp_branch()`.
   - SSH in. Capture remote prior state: `REMOTE_PREV=$(git -C /workspace/Reigh-Worker rev-parse HEAD)`.
   - `cd /workspace/Reigh-Worker && git fetch origin && git checkout <branch> && git pull && uv sync --locked --extra cuda124`.
   - `kill_supervisor_and_worker(ssh)`.
   - `launch_worker(ssh, '/workspace/Reigh-Worker', [...same args as Variant A including --reigh-access-token...])`.
   - `wait_until_ready` → run matrix → aggregate report.
3. **Cleanup (success):** SSH `git checkout $REMOTE_PREV && uv sync --locked --extra cuda124`, relaunch worker with prior args (best effort — document limitation), then `cleanup_temp_branch(preserve=False)`, `restore_local_ref(prev_ref)`, `guarded_terminate(pod_id, ..., no_terminate=True)` **by default for Variant B** (we don't own the pod) unless `--terminate` explicitly set.
4. **Cleanup (failure):** leave remote pod on the temp branch, `cleanup_temp_branch(preserve=True)` prints the branch name, still `restore_local_ref` on dev machine. Never terminate on failure.

**Deliverables:** `git_ops.py`, `variant_b.py`.
**Validation:** `test_variant_b_safety_gate_blocks_on_in_flight_task` unit test; git_ops unit test that does the branch lifecycle in a tmp repo (not against `origin`).

---

## Phase 5: Task Matrix Runner

### Step 17: Matrix definition (`reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/matrix.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Define** a list of 8 `MatrixCase(name, task_type, fixture_path_or_inline_params)`:
   - `travel_orchestrator_wan2_1seg` — inline params (no fixture).
   - `travel_orchestrator_ltx` — inline params with LTX model flag.
   - `individual_travel_segment` — fixture `wan22_i2v_individual_segment`.
   - `qwen_image_style` — inline params with `style_reference_image_url`.
   - `qwen_image_t2i` — fixture `qwen_image_basic`.
   - `qwen_image_2512` — inline params (newer checkpoint).
   - `qwen_image_edit` — fixture `qwen_image_edit_basic`.
   - `z_image_turbo_i2i` — fixture `z_image_turbo_i2i_basic` (NOTE: `_i2i` suffix per `WGP_TASK_TYPES`).
2. **Implement** `run_matrix(db, project_id, cases, timeout_sec) -> list[TaskResult]` that, for each case: spoofs the task, polls until complete, records result. Continues on individual failures — overall report is per-task, not all-or-nothing.

### Step 18: Report writer (`reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/report.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Write** `write_report(results, variant, pod_id, out_dir)` producing `report.json` (machine) and `report.md` (human, with pass/fail counts, per-task elapsed, `output_location` URLs, `generation_ids`, log excerpts on failure). Runs dir gitignored.

**Deliverables:** `matrix.py`, `report.py`, 8 cases wired.
**Validation:** Matrix can be dry-run-loaded and printed without DB/RunPod access.

---

## Phase 6: Smoke + First Real Run

### Step 19: Smoke test (`reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/smoke.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Compose** a smoke script that (a) resolves token against real Supabase, (b) loads all 8 matrix fixtures/inline templates and validates JSON shape, (c) optionally runs Variant B safety gate against a user-supplied `--pod-id`, (d) never creates pods, never inserts tasks, never pushes branches.
2. **Exit codes** 0/1 for CI-friendliness.

### Step 20: First real run — Variant A (no code changes)
**Scope:** Small (operational)
1. **Run** `python -m scripts.live_test.variant_a --no-terminate`. Capture report. Iterate on any harness bugs. Promote to terminate-on-success once 8/8 pass.
2. **Do not** run Variant B against any production pod. When ready, spawn a fresh test pod, let Variant A finish standard install, then run Variant B `--pod-id <that pod>`.

**Deliverables:** Two green run reports under `scripts/live_test/runs/<ts>/`.
**Validation:** 8/8 tasks `Complete` with non-empty `generation_ids`.

---

## Phase 7: Cleanup

### Step 21: Gitignore + hygiene (`.gitignore`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/runs/` and `reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/stage1_findings.md` to `.gitignore`.
2. **Verify** no temp branches linger: `git -C reigh-worker ls-remote origin 'refs/heads/live-test/*'` returns empty. Manually delete any strays.
3. **Confirm** the test token is only loaded from env (`REIGH_LIVE_TEST_TOKEN`) or an untracked `.env.live-test` file — not hardcoded anywhere in the harness.

**Deliverables:** Clean repo state; no orchestrator production files touched.
**Validation:** `git status` on orchestrator, worker, and app repos shows only additions under `scripts/live_test/` + the `.gitignore` entry.

---

## Execution Order

1. **Phase 1** first — resolving schema/routing/fixtures unblocks everything else and is zero-code risk.
2. **Phase 2** primitives + unit tests — cheapest validation layer; catches drift before any GPU minutes are burned.
3. **Phase 5 (Step 17 matrix definition)** can land in parallel with Phase 3 since it only depends on primitives.
4. **Phase 3 (Variant A)** before Phase 4 (Variant B) — lower blast radius and exercises the same launch command builder Variant B needs to construct.
5. **Phase 6** smoke + real runs after all code lands; Variant A first, Variant B against a fresh pod only.
6. **Phase 7** cleanup after a green real run.

## Validation Order

1. **Unit tests** for primitives (`scripts/live_test/tests/`) — seconds, no network.
2. **Dry-run** both drivers — resolves token, loads fixtures, prints plan. No GPU, no inserts.
3. **Smoke** — hits real Supabase for token resolution + optional safety gate against a user-supplied pod.
4. **First real Variant A run** with `--no-terminate` so failed pods stay live for SSH debugging.
5. **Promote** to terminate-on-success once 8/8 pass.
6. **Then** Variant B against a freshly spawned-and-standard-installed pod (never a production pod on first run).
7. **Finally** confirm existing orchestrator tests still pass (`uv run pytest reigh-worker-orchestrator/tests/`).

      "criterion": "Variant B git ops push to temp branch `live-test/<ts>-<sha>` only \u2014 never to `main`, never force-push, never `--no-verify`; verifiable by inspecting git_ops.py",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Variant B on success deletes the temp remote branch and restores the local submodule to its pre-run ref; on failure preserves the branch and logs its name",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Variant B kills BOTH supervisor.py AND worker.py before relaunching \u2014 verifiable in `ssh_bootstrap.kill_supervisor_and_worker`",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "No files under `reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/runpod/` (worker_startup.template.sh, startup_script.py, api.py, ssh.py, lifecycle.py) are modified \u2014 verifiable via `git diff`",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "No files under the `reigh-worker` submodule source tree are modified (source/ untouched) \u2014 verifiable via `git -C reigh-worker status`",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Existing orchestrator unit tests (test_startup_script.py and others under `reigh-worker-orchestrator/tests/`) still pass unchanged",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "New primitive unit tests (`scripts/live_test/tests/test_primitives.py`) pass, including: token_resolver, task_spoofer, completion_poller, safety_gate, terminate_guard",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Task matrix reporting is per-task (8 rows) with `final_status`, `output_location`, `generation_ids`, and `elapsed_sec` \u2014 NOT all-or-nothing; a 7/8 run still writes a complete report",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "First real Variant A run completes with 8/8 tasks at `status='Complete'` AND each row has at least one corresponding `generations` row in the project+time window",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "First real Variant B run (against freshly spawned test pod, not a production pod) completes with 8/8 tasks Complete + generations verified",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Test token `3HKcoLeJAFFfTFFeRV6Eu7Lq` is not hardcoded anywhere in the harness source \u2014 loaded only from env var",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "After cleanup, `git ls-remote origin 'refs/heads/live-test/*'` on banodoco/Reigh-Worker returns empty",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Harness primitive modules each stay under ~200 lines and have a single responsibility (token_resolver, task_spoofer, completion_poller, ssh_bootstrap, heartbeat_waiter, safety_gate, terminate_guard)",
      "priority": "should"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Variant A and Variant B share the same matrix runner and task_spoofer code \u2014 no duplication of the 8-task list across drivers",
      "priority": "should"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Dry-run path on both drivers works without any Supabase or RunPod credentials beyond read-only token resolution",
      "priority": "should"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Per-task completion poller timeout is configurable via CLI (default 20 min)",
      "priority": "should"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Report output is human-readable markdown + machine-parseable JSON",
      "priority": "should"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Harness reuses `DatabaseClient`, `SSHClient`, `create_pod_and_wait`, `get_pod_ssh_details`, `get_running_tasks_for_worker` from the orchestrator package without copying code",
      "priority": "should"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Stage 1 probe findings documented in `stage1_findings.md` (untracked) with schema state + resolved user_id + pod config defaults",
      "priority": "info"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Run reports archived at `scripts/live_test/runs/<ts>/` for post-hoc debugging",
      "priority": "info"
    }
  ],
  "assumptions": [
    "user_api_tokens uses plain `token` column in production (confirmed by migration 20250713000008). If production diverges, Stage 1 probe will catch it and the token resolver will need a SHA256 fallback branch.",
    "The PAT path in `claim-next-task` (index.ts:173-208) means a worker authenticating with `--reigh-access-token` claims only tasks in projects owned by that token's user. Combined with a dedicated `live-test` project + `params.live_test=true` marker, production workers cannot accidentally claim test tasks and vice versa. No additional routing isolation needed.",
    "Variant A default: terminate pod on success, keep on failure. Variant B default: do NOT terminate on success or failure (we don't own the pod).",
    "`--idle-release-minutes 1` is the safe default for both variants; `0` is available as an override. Matches brief's 'small value' fallback.",
    "Missing fixtures (qwen_image_style, qwen_image_2512, both travel_orchestrator variants) will be hand-authored from task handler param signatures read in Stage 1. No new asset uploads needed \u2014 references use existing public URLs from the captured fixtures where possible.",
    "The test token `3HKcoLeJAFFfTFFeRV6Eu7Lq` is loaded from env var `REIGH_LIVE_TEST_TOKEN` (set by the user manually or from untracked `.env.live-test`), never committed, never in CLI defaults.",
    "Variant A clones from `main` branch of `banodoco/Reigh-Worker`. If the user needs a different ref, a `--ref` flag can be added \u2014 not default behavior.",
    "Variant B 'spawn new and take over' uses `create_pod_and_wait` with the production startup template first (to match 'existing pod with /workspace/Reigh-Worker/ installed'), then immediately performs the takeover flow. This is a distinct mode from Variant A's custom-dir fresh install.",
    "Report output lives at `reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/runs/<ts>/{variant_a,variant_b}.{json,md}` and is gitignored.",
    "Unit tests live under `scripts/live_test/tests/` (new dir) so they don't collide with existing `reigh-worker-orchestrator/tests/`. They'll be runnable via `uv run pytest scripts/live_test/tests/` from the orchestrator repo.",
    "Existing orchestrator unit tests (test_startup_script.py, DatabaseClient/SSHClient/lifecycle tests) are not modified and will continue to pass \u2014 the harness only adds new files, never mutates shared primitives."
  ],
  "structure_warnings": []

  "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-007",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-008",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-018",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-106",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-107",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }
  ],

      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the package is internally inconsistent. the plan body says to keep `activepairdata` in `usestate` and make phase 2 optional, but the attached metadata and success criteria still describe the previous derive-via-`usememo` / remove-`setactivepairdata` / simplify-`onpairclick` plan. that means the approved-plan requirements are only partially coherent as presented.",

      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the linux distro note is documentation-only; the generated install command in step 7 still runs `apt-get install python3.10-venv python3.10-dev ffmpeg` without any pre-check that the package exists. users on ubuntu 24.04+ who ignore the doc and try to copy-paste the command will hit a confusing `e: unable to locate package python3.10-venv` from apt rather than a targeted error from commandutils.ts. a one-line detection pre-check (e.g., `apt-cache show python3.10-venv >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo 'install deadsnakes ppa first \u2014 see readme'; exit 1; }`) would convert the silent failure into an actionable error, but the plan does not add this.",

      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the non-swallowing helper `update_worker_phase_strict` is a new shell function, but phase 1 step 2 introduces it only as pseudo-code. there is no concrete note that the helper must handle supabase http non-2xx responses as failures (curl returns 0 even on http 4xx/5xx by default unless `-f` or `--fail-with-body` is used). if the implementer ports `update_worker_phase` verbatim with only `|| return 0` removed, a 500 from supabase would still report success because curl's exit code is 0. the plan should specify `curl --fail` or explicit http status code checking in the strict variant.",

    {
      "id": "DEBT-109",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }
  ],

  {
    "subsystem": "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct",
    "total_occurrences": 5,
    "plan_count": 2,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-007",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-008",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-018",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-106",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-107",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      }
    ]
  },

        Project directory:
        /Users/user_c042661f/Documents/reigh-workspace

        Engineering brief produced from the codebase and task details:

        ### Task Summary
        Design and implement a live end-to-end test harness for the Reigh platform that exercises the individual-user worker code path (not orchestrator-managed scaling) against real RunPod GPUs and real Supabase tasks, scoped to the user who owns Reigh access [REDACTED] Deliver two driver variants (A: fresh pod + fresh clone into a non-default directory name; B: update an existing Reigh-Worker pod from a local working copy pushed to a temp branch), both running the same 8-task matrix, both verifying generations rows, with reusable primitives (token-to-user resolver, task spoofer, poll-until-complete, SSH/pod helpers borrowed from orchestrator).

        ### Key Evidence
        - Orchestrator's existing startup template hardcodes --supabase-access-token "$SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY" and --wgp-profile 1; does NOT pass --reigh-access-token or --idle-release-minutes. 'buildWorkerLaunchLine-equivalent' in the task means we must build a new launch command from scratch — cannot reuse render_startup_script() as-is for either variant. (source: reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/runpod/worker_startup.template.sh:355-366; startup_script.py:38-65; relevance: high)
- The worker DOES accept --reigh-access-token (server.py:226-228), --idle-release-minutes (parsed via idle_release.add_cli_args line 229, default 15.0, triggers exit code 143 when idle queue polling exceeds the duration), and --wgp-profile (int, 1-5). (source: reigh-worker/source/runtime/worker/server.py:211-245; source/runtime/worker/idle_release.py; relevance: high)
- terminate_pod(pod_id, api_key) has NO opt-out flag in current code — it is a bare wrapper around runpod.terminate_pod(). The task's 'opt-out flag' requirement means the harness needs its own guard (CLI flag --no-terminate or env var REIGH_LIVE_TEST_NO_TERMINATE) that the driver honors. (source: reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/runpod/api.py:223-229; relevance: high)
- SCHEMA DISCREPANCY: task says 'via user_api_tokens.jti_hash', but migration 20250713000008 DROPPED jti_hash and expires_at. Current schema is (id, user_id, token [plain text, unique], label). Token resolution is: select user_id from user_api_tokens where [REDACTED]. No SHA256 hashing. MUST be verified in stage 1 before building the resolver. (source: reigh-app/supabase/migrations/20250711000001_create_user_api_tokens.sql; 20250713000008_*.sql; functions/generate-pat/index.ts:9-14; relevance: high)
- Tasks are scoped to a user indirectly via projects: tasks.project_id -> projects.id -> projects.user_id. There is NO tasks.user_id column. To 'spoof tasks scoped to the user the token resolves to' the harness must: (a) resolve token->user_id, (b) find or create a project owned by that user, (c) insert task with that project_id. Task status enum values EXACT: 'Queued','In Progress','Complete','Failed','Cancelled'. (source: reigh-app/supabase/migrations/20250100000000_create_base_schema.sql:4,25-37; relevance: high)
- Task claim path: worker POSTs {SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/claim-next-task with {worker_id, run_type:'gpu', same_model_only:true}. Edge function handles Queued->In Progress. Completion via complete-task / update-task-status edge functions which also write to generations. 'Verify generation was produced' means polling tasks.status='Complete' AND querying generations table by project_id + timestamp window (no direct task_id FK on generations). (source: reigh-worker/source/core/db/task_claim.py:163-257; reigh-app/supabase/functions/complete-task/generation-handlers.ts; relevance: high)
- SSH helper already built: gpu_orchestrator.runpod.ssh.SSHClient (paramiko), authenticates via private key env var > private key file > password. DatabaseClient.execute_command_on_worker(runpod_id, command, timeout) wraps it with pod SSH details lookup. Both directly reusable. (source: reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/runpod/ssh.py:11-107; client.py:175-203; relevance: high)
- Heartbeat detection: worker POSTs /rest/v1/rpc/func_update_worker_heartbeat (or func_worker_heartbeat_with_logs) every ~20s updating workers.last_heartbeat. 'Ready' = (a) workers.last_heartbeat > created_at + 5s, (b) metadata.startup_phase='ready', (c) metadata.ready_for_tasks=true. Poll workers table by worker_id (pod_id) for fresh heartbeat. (source: reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/worker_state.py:150-170; database.py:299-315; reigh-worker/source/runtime/worker/guardian.py:130-154; relevance: high)
- Workdir discovery in template.sh is hardcoded to $WORKSPACE_DIR/Reigh-Worker (primary) or Headless-Wan2GP (fallback). For Variant A's 'NEW name Reigh-Worker-LiveTest' requirement, must write a custom startup script (or run install via SSHClient.execute_command) that clones into the custom directory. Cannot rely on the existing template or build_launch_command(). (source: reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/runpod/worker_startup.template.sh:167-183; startup_script.py:12-25; relevance: high)
- Variant B 'in-flight task' safety check: reject takeover if workers.metadata.ready_for_tasks=true and any tasks row has worker_id=<pod_worker_id> with status='In Progress', OR workers.last_heartbeat fresh (<60s). Use DatabaseClient.get_running_tasks_for_worker(worker_id) which already implements this. (source: reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/database.py:346-366; relevance: high)
- Reference test payloads already exist at reigh-worker/artifacts/worker-matrix/*/captured_generation_task.json — qwen_image_basic, z_image_turbo_basic, wan22_i2v_individual_segment and others. These are the authoritative fixtures for the task spoofer's payload shapes. Read and mutate them rather than hand-authoring. (source: reigh-worker/artifacts/worker-matrix/20260316T135006000511Z/*, 20260316T140127343314Z/*; relevance: high)
- WGP_TASK_TYPES frozenset enumerates exact worker-recognized task_type strings: 'qwen_image','qwen_image_edit','qwen_image_style','qwen_image_2512','z_image_turbo','z_image_turbo_i2i' (I2I variant suffix). 'travel_orchestrator' and 'individual_travel_segment' are orchestrator task types handled separately. Task says 'z_image_turbo I2I' which maps to z_image_turbo_i2i. (source: reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/task_types.py:26-46; relevance: high)
- Existing reusable scaffold: reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/create_test_task.py already inserts {task_type, project_id, params, status:'Queued'} via DatabaseClient.supabase.table('tasks').insert(...). Task spoofer should extend this pattern. (source: reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/create_test_task.py:18-74; relevance: medium)
- Prior plan 'build-a-spoofable-end-to-end-20260410-1456' and 'look-at-how-we-create-tasks-20260410-1842' exist in .megaplan/plans/. Worth skimming in stage 1 for reusable primitives or decisions. (source: .megaplan/plans/build-a-spoofable-end-to-end-20260410-1456/; .megaplan/plans/look-at-how-we-create-tasks-20260410-1842/; relevance: medium)
- Worker supervisor restarts on idle exit code 143 (supervisor.py:1-81). For Variant B 'cleanly restart worker': killing worker.py alone is NOT enough — supervisor will resurrect it with OLD CLI args. Harness must kill supervisor too, then launch a fresh supervisor with new --reigh-access-token flag. (source: reigh-worker/source/runtime/supervisor.py:1-81; relevance: high)
- Variant B pushes local working copy to banodoco/Reigh-Worker on a temp branch. reigh-worker is a git submodule of the workspace. Requires: (a) valid git credentials on dev machine, (b) commit of uncommitted local changes to the temp branch, (c) cleanup deletes temp branch post-run. Force-push never needed. Must NOT modify main. (source: reigh-worker/ submodule; remote banodoco/Reigh-Worker.git per worker_startup.template.sh:181; relevance: high)
