        ### Test Expectations
        - [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::variant_a::fresh_install: create_pod_and_wait, SSH in, clone banodoco/Reigh-Worker into /workspace/Reigh-Worker-LiveTest, run install (apt + uv sync --locked --extra cuda124), launch worker.py with [REDACTED] --wgp-profile 3 --idle-release-minutes 0, poll workers table until fresh heartbeat + metadata.startup_phase='ready'. Run 8-task matrix via task spoofer, poll tasks until Complete, assert generations rows exist for project_id within time window. Report per-task pass/fail with output_location URL. Finally terminate_pod unless --no-terminate set.
- [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::variant_b::update_existing: Target existing pod with /workspace/Reigh-Worker/ (or spawn new + standard install + take over). Safety check rejects if any tasks In Progress for pod worker OR workers.last_heartbeat < 60s. Commit local reigh-worker working copy to temp branch live-test/<ts>-<hash> on banodoco/Reigh-Worker, push. SSH in, git fetch + checkout temp branch + pull, uv sync --locked --extra cuda124. Kill supervisor+worker, re-launch with --reigh-access-token. Wait for heartbeat. Run same 8-task matrix, assert Complete + generations. On success, restore pod to previous branch/commit and delete temp branch. On failure, leave branch for debugging.
- [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::task_matrix::travel_orchestrator_wan2_1seg: Inserts travel_orchestrator task with 1-segment Wan 2.2 i2v payload; verifies orchestrator decomposes into individual_travel_segment, segment completes, generations row written.
- [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::task_matrix::travel_orchestrator_ltx: Same as above but with LTX model variant in segment params.
- [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::task_matrix::individual_travel_segment: Direct individual_travel_segment task (bypassing orchestrator); payload cloned from artifacts/worker-matrix/wan22_i2v_individual_segment fixture.
- [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::task_matrix::qwen_image_style: qwen_image_style task with a style reference image URL; verifies worker downloads ref, runs style transfer, writes output.
- [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::task_matrix::qwen_image_t2i: qwen_image text-to-image task; payload cloned from qwen_image_basic fixture.
- [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::task_matrix::qwen_image_2512: qwen_image_2512 task variant (newer model checkpoint).
- [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::task_matrix::qwen_image_edit: qwen_image_edit with input image; verifies edit pipeline writes generation.
- [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::task_matrix::z_image_turbo_i2i: z_image_turbo_i2i (I2I variant with _i2i suffix, NOT bare z_image_turbo) with input image; payload derived from z_image_turbo_basic fixture mutated to i2i shape.
- [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::primitives::token_user_resolver: Given [REDACTED] returns the correct user_id. Verifies schema state matches (plain token column, not jti_hash).
- [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::primitives::variant_b_safety_gate: Given pod with In Progress task OR fresh heartbeat, Variant B driver refuses to take over and exits non-zero. Unit-testable with mock DatabaseClient.
- [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::primitives::terminate_opt_out: With REIGH_LIVE_TEST_NO_TERMINATE=1 or --no-terminate, driver completes and does NOT call terminate_pod (verifiable by mock).
- [pass_to_pass] existing::tests/gpu_orchestrator/runpod/test_startup_script.py: Existing unit tests for render_startup_script / build_launch_command. Must continue to pass — harness adds NEW builders without mutating existing ones.
- [pass_to_pass] existing::reigh-worker-orchestrator tests: Unit tests for DatabaseClient, SSHClient, lifecycle — harness must not modify shared primitives in breaking ways.

        ### Constraints
        - MUST NOT modify the orchestrator's existing worker_startup.template.sh, render_startup_script, or any production lifecycle code path. Harness is additive — new files under reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/.
- MUST NOT interfere with production orchestrator runs. Spoofed tasks MUST be scoped to the test user ([REDACTED]). Stage 1 must confirm how task routing works — if pool-wide, use a dedicated live-test project and a params.live_test=true marker so a production worker claiming a test task is harmless.
- MUST NOT force-push or push to main. Variant B pushes to temp branch live-test/<timestamp>-<short-hash> and deletes on success. Never --no-verify, never amend shared commits.
- MUST NOT clobber pods doing real user work. Variant B safety gate is mandatory: reject takeover if pod has any tasks In Progress for worker_id, or fresh (<60s) heartbeat.
- MUST include an opt-out flag (env REIGH_LIVE_TEST_NO_TERMINATE=1 and/or --no-terminate CLI flag) that skips terminate_pod() on both normal-exit and exception paths.
- MUST resolve token->user via the CURRENT schema (plain `token` column). If stage 1 discovers production still uses jti_hash, fall back to SHA256(token) hex. Do not guess — verify first.
- MUST use exact task_status enum capitalization: 'Queued', 'In Progress', 'Complete', 'Failed', 'Cancelled'.
- MUST use z_image_turbo_i2i (with _i2i suffix) for the I2I matrix entry, not z_image_turbo.
- Task matrix failures reported per-task (8 rows), not all-or-nothing. 7/8 passing still produces a useful report.
- Per-task completion polling must have a configurable timeout (suggest 20 min default) and capture final status + tasks.output_location + generations.id list within project scope + time window.
- Variant B post-run cleanup: restore pod to previous branch/commit via git checkout <prev>, THEN delete temp remote branch. On failure, preserve temp branch and print its name.
- Secrets (RUNPOD_API_KEY, SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY, SSH private key) loaded from existing .env locations (reigh-worker-orchestrator/.env) — never hardcoded or prompted for.

        ### Suggested Approach
        Stage 1 (prep/finalize unknowns): (a) Verify user_api_tokens schema state in production by reading migrations in order and running a read-only schema probe via DatabaseClient — confirm plain token column vs jti_hash. (b) Confirm how individual-user worker routing scopes task claims — is there a user/worker identity filter in claim-next-task, or is the queue pool-wide? If pool-wide, create a dedicated 'live-test' project under the target user and tag tasks with params.live_test=true so any stray claim by a real worker is harmless. (c) Skim .megaplan/plans/build-a-spoofable-end-to-end-20260410-1456/ and look-at-how-we-create-tasks-20260410-1842/ for reusable primitives. (d) Confirm RunPod GPU type + template_id (read orchestrator config defaults in config.py). (e) Pin the 8 payload shapes by reading artifacts/worker-matrix/* + qwen_image_style/qwen_image_2512/qwen_image_edit handler param expectations. Deliverables: stage1_findings.md. Files touched: none (read-only). Risks: production schema may differ from checked-in migrations. Validation: schema probe returns expected columns; routing confirmed from claim-next-task edge source.

Stage 2 (shared primitives): Create reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/ package: (i) token_resolver.py — token->user_id using confirmed schema. (ii) project_for_user.py — get-or-create a dedicated live-test project. (iii) task_spoofer.py — insert_task(task_type, params, project_id) loading reference payloads from artifacts/worker-matrix/ and mutating per test. (iv) completion_poller.py — poll_until_complete(task_id, timeout_sec) returns (final_status, output_location, generation_ids) via tasks + generations tables. (v) ssh_bootstrap.py — wraps SSHClient with clone_repo_into(dir, url, branch), run_uv_sync(workdir), kill_supervisor_and_worker(), launch_worker(workdir, cli_args, worker_id, env). (vi) heartbeat_waiter.py — poll workers table until fresh heartbeat + phase=ready. (vii) terminate_guard.py — honors env/CLI opt-out. Deliverables: 7 primitive modules + unit tests with mocked DatabaseClient/SSHClient. Files touched: new under scripts/live_test/. Risks: drift from orchestrator helper semantics. Validation: unit tests pass; ruff/mypy clean.

Stage 3 (Variant A driver — fresh install): variant_a.py calls create_pod_and_wait (reusing orchestrator GPU config + template + env_vars, injecting REIGH_ACCESS_TOKEN env var), waits for SSH, runs apt deps + uv installer + `git clone https://github.com/banodoco/Reigh-Worker.git /workspace/Reigh-Worker-LiveTest`, cd, `uv sync --locked --extra cuda124`, then launches worker via `nohup uv run --python 3.10 --extra cuda124 python worker.py --supabase-url <url> [REDACTED] --worker <pod_id> --wgp-profile 3 --idle-release-minutes 0 > logs/startup.log 2>&1 &`. Calls heartbeat_waiter, then runs the 8-task matrix via task_spoofer + completion_poller, aggregates results, prints report, calls terminate_guard. Deliverables: variant_a.py + CLI entry. Risks: RunPod timeouts; apt mirror issues; uv network hiccups. Validation: dry-run passes; first real run with --no-terminate succeeds on at least qwen_image (fast task).

Stage 4 (Variant B driver — update existing): variant_b.py takes --pod-id (existing) or spawns + does standard install first. Runs safety_gate. Commits local reigh-worker tree: `git -C reigh-worker checkout -b live-test/<ts>-<sha>`, `git add`, `git commit`, `git push -u origin <branch>`. SSH in, `cd /workspace/Reigh-Worker && git fetch origin && PREV_REF=$(git rev-parse HEAD) && git checkout <branch> && git pull && uv sync --locked --extra cuda124`. kill_supervisor_and_worker, re-launch with --reigh-access-token. heartbeat_waiter. Same 8-task matrix + reporting. On cleanup: SSH `git checkout $PREV_REF`, delete temp remote branch. On failure: preserve temp branch, print name. Deliverables: variant_b.py. Risks: submodule git state; credentials; supervisor races; clobbering real work. Validation: safety gate unit test; first real run on freshly-spawned test pod, not a production pod.

Stage 5 (dry-run/smoke-test harness itself): Before touching a real GPU: unit-test pass on primitives with mocked DatabaseClient + SSHClient. Add --dry-run flag to both variants that (a) resolves token, (b) runs Variant B safety gate against a real pod, (c) loads every payload fixture and validates shape against handler params, (d) does NOT spawn pods or push branches. Catches schema drift and missing secrets without burning GPU minutes. Deliverables: --dry-run on both drivers + scripts/live_test/smoke.py. Validation: smoke prints expected plan for both variants.

Stage 6 (first real run): Run Variant A first (lower blast radius). Use --no-terminate on first attempt so failed pods stay up for SSH debugging. Iterate until all 8 tasks pass, then promote to terminate-on-success. Then Variant B against a freshly-spawned-and-standard-installed pod (not a production pod). Only use reused persistent-volume pods once the driver is proven. Deliverables: two successful run reports (JSON + log) in .megaplan/plans/<slug>/runs/. Risks: real worker bugs surfaced (good!), test infra bugs (iterate). Validation: report shows 8/8 pass.

Stage 7 (cleanup/documentation): Purge lingering temp branches on banodoco/Reigh-Worker (`git ls-remote | grep live-test/` should be empty). Update reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/README or a new docs/live_test.md ONLY if user requests docs — per project policy, do not proactively create docs. Add .gitignore entry for report output directory. NEVER check in the test token — load from env or untracked local file. Validation: stale branches purged; .gitignore updated.

File layout: reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/ (alongside existing create_test_task.py, test_supabase.py) — naturally shares DatabaseClient + runpod primitives without cross-repo imports. Keep reigh-worker itself untouched — no worker-side changes needed.

Top risks: (1) Task routing: if tasks aren't actually scoped per-user, test tasks could be stolen by real workers or vice versa — stage 1 MUST resolve this before stage 2 work starts. (2) --idle-release-minutes 0 may cause the worker to exit before claiming the first task if queue polling is slow — consider 1 minute with a bumped idle_onboarding_grace_seconds. (3) Supervisor restart: killing worker.py alone causes relaunch with OLD args — must kill supervisor first. (4) Variant B git push requires credentials on the dev machine. (5) terminate_pod has no real opt-out in orchestrator code, so the flag must be enforced in the harness's own code path — honor it on both normal-exit AND exception paths.

        The engineering brief above was produced by analyzing the codebase. Use it as a strong starting point, but the suggested approach is a hypothesis — verify it's the best fix, not just a valid one.

        Idea:
Design a robust end-to-end live test harness for the Reigh platform that exercises the individual-user worker path (NOT the orchestrator-managed worker path) against a specific user identified by Reigh access [REDACTED] Two variants of one live test, both targeting a real RunPod GPU, both creating real spoof tasks in Supabase for that specific user, both waiting for actual task completion and verifying generations were produced end-to-end. Variant A 'Fresh Install': spin up a new RunPod pod using the orchestrator's create_pod_and_wait approach, SSH in and clone reigh-worker into a folder with a NEW name (NOT Reigh-Worker, e.g. Reigh-Worker-LiveTest), run buildWorkerLaunchLine-equivalent install/launch with [REDACTED] --wgp-profile 3 --idle-release-minutes 0, wait for heartbeat, insert spoof tasks in Supabase scoped to the user that the token resolves to (via user_api_tokens.jti_hash), poll until each task reaches Complete with generations row, report pass/fail per task with output URLs, terminate pod via terminate_pod() with opt-out flag. Variant B 'Update Existing': target an existing pod with /workspace/Reigh-Worker/ installed (or spawn+standard-install then take over), push current local working copy of reigh-worker/ to a temporary branch on banodoco/Reigh-Worker, SSH in, git fetch+checkout+pull, uv sync --locked, cleanly restart worker with --reigh-access-token for test user, run same task matrix, on success optionally delete temp branch and restore pod to previous branch/state. Variant B must not clobber pods doing real user work — include safety check for in-flight tasks. Task matrix (one of each): travel_orchestrator standard Wan 2 images 1 segment, travel_orchestrator LTX variant, individual_travel_segment single segment, qwen_image_style with style ref, qwen_image text-to-image, qwen_image_2512, qwen_image_edit, z_image_turbo I2I. Stages: (1) prep/finalize unknowns, (2) build shared primitives (token→user resolver, task spoofer, poll-until-complete, SSH helpers reused from orchestrator), (3) build Variant A driver, (4) build Variant B driver, (5) dry-run/smoke-test the harness itself, (6) first real run, (7) cleanup/documentation. For each stage: concrete deliverables, files to touch, risks, validation.

User notes and answers:
- User decisions on blockers and open questions (authoritative — supersedes earlier plan choices):

BLOCKER 1 (isolation): [REDACTED] is ALREADY a dedicated test user account. It has no production work and is explicitly a test token. User confirmed we can proceed using this token directly. Still do a defensive preflight check (abort if any unexpected non-test Queued/In-Progress task exists for the user, as a safety net), but no need to pause production, create a new test user, or use service-role for claims.

BLOCKER 2 (readiness signal): Option (a). Inject BOTH the PAT ([REDACTED]) AND SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY into the pod. The worker must still run the INDIVIDUAL-USER PAT path for task claims (WORKER_DB_CLIENT_AUTH_MODE=worker) — this is a hard requirement. The service role key is injected purely so heartbeat RPCs work and the standard ready_for_tasks detection path is available. Do NOT switch to service-role claim path.

BLOCKER 3 (Variant B takeover safety gate): Orchestrator's call — use option (a). Implement a PAT-aware in-flight check that queries tasks for the target user filtered by status (In Progress) and generation_started_at (recent window) to detect any live PAT work on the pod before takeover. Do NOT rely on the worker_id column (PAT claims don't stamp it).

OPEN QUESTIONS (all answered):
- Q1 driver location: reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/ (INSIDE reigh-worker repo, NOT reigh-worker-orchestrator). The driver imports SSH / runpod primitives FROM reigh-worker-orchestrator but lives in the worker repo.
- Q2 credentials: env vars RUNPOD_API_KEY, SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY, REIGH_LIVE_TEST_TOKEN (never committed, loaded from .env or shell env).
- Q4 LTX model: resolve from reigh-worker source (travel_guidance.py / model registry) in Stage 1. Pick an actual supported LTX model name by reading the code, not guessing.
- Q5 individual_travel_segment standalone: existing handler already supports it as part of the travel flow. Use it directly — no synthetic parent needed.
- Q6 timeouts: longer to absorb model/asset downloads on fresh pods. Default travel_orchestrator 40 min, individual_travel_segment 25 min, image tasks 15 min. All configurable via flag.
- Q7 failure handling: run ALL tasks in the matrix regardless of individual failures. Emit one consolidated pass/fail report at the end showing per-task status, output URLs, durations, and error summaries.
- Q8 fixtures: pick good anchor images — 2 high-contrast scenes with clear subjects. Bake them into the driver as the default set, but make them swappable via config.

DEFAULTS (confirmed, not overridden):
- Flag 4 launch path: use run_worker.py via the supervisor (the canonical buildWorkerLaunchLine path from reigh-app/src/shared/components/SettingsModal/commandUtils.ts). Do NOT shell out to worker.py directly. Variant B worker restarts must kill BOTH supervisor.py AND its child worker process, then relaunch via run_worker.py.
- Flag 5 idle release: use --idle-release-minutes 0 which disables idle release (<=0 disables per source/runtime/worker/idle_release.py:41-43). Correct default.

VARIANT B TEARDOWN (B1/B2/B3):
- B1 pod restore: After the test finishes (pass or fail), restore the pod to its pre-takeover state — git checkout the original branch (captured at start of takeover) and relaunch the original supervisor+worker process. EXCEPTION: if the pre-takeover branch was itself a scratch/throwaway branch (e.g., another live-test/* branch or a detached HEAD), skip restoration and leave the worker stopped.
- B2 termination: Variant B defaults to --no-terminate TRUE. We do not own the pod, so never terminate by default; operator must explicitly opt in with a flag if they want termination.
- B3 Variant B sub-modes: Variant B supports TWO operating modes and must implement both:
  * B-existing: operator passes in an existing pod ID; driver assumes a Reigh-Worker install is already present at /workspace/Reigh-Worker.
  * B-fresh-takeover: driver spawns a fresh pod using create_pod_and_wait(), lets the standard production orchestrator startup template run (install + worker start), waits for the standard install to complete and the worker to come online, THEN performs the branch push + git pull + worker restart takeover. Same task matrix runs after takeover in both modes.

GLOBAL NOTES:
- Variant A folder name: use Reigh-Worker-LiveTest (or similar distinct name) at /workspace to avoid collision with any existing Reigh-Worker install.
- The plan must treat the individual-user PAT path as the PRIMARY worker auth mode for all test runs. Service role key is only present to unblock heartbeat writes and metadata updates, never for task claim calls.
- The driver is a single Python entrypoint with --variant fresh|update and mode flags, reusing SSH/pod primitives from reigh-worker-orchestrator.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Live E2E Test Harness for Individual-User Worker Path

## Overview

Build an additive live test harness at `reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/` (inside the reigh-worker repo, importing SSH/pod primitives from the sibling `reigh-worker-orchestrator` package) that exercises the individual-user PAT worker code path against a real RunPod GPU and real Supabase, scoped to the dedicated test-user token `3HKcoLeJAFFfTFFeRV6Eu7Lq`. One Python entrypoint with `--variant fresh|update` and Variant B sub-modes.

**Architecture corrections from gate feedback (per user notes + verified code reads):**

1. **Launch path is `run_worker.py` (supervisor entrypoint)**, NOT `worker.py`. Matches the canonical builder at `reigh-app/src/shared/components/SettingsModal/commandUtils.ts`. Variant B kills supervisor + child worker then relaunches via `run_worker.py`.
2. **Dual credential injection**: pod receives BOTH `REIGH_ACCESS_TOKEN` (the test PAT) AND `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`. Worker claims tasks via the PAT path (`WORKER_DB_CLIENT_AUTH_MODE=worker`, a hard requirement); service-role is present only so heartbeat RPCs (`func_update_worker_heartbeat`) and `update-worker-model` succeed, since those grants are service-role-only per `reigh-app/supabase/migrations/20260311110000_restrict_worker_heartbeat_rpc_grants.sql`.
3. **`--idle-release-minutes 0` is correct as-specified**. Per `reigh-worker/source/runtime/worker/idle_release.py` (`should_release()` returns `False` when `idle_minutes <= 0`), `0` DISABLES idle release, which is the intended behavior: test pods stay alive through slow queue polls. This corrects the prior plan's wrong inversion.
4. **Readiness check drops `ready_for_tasks`**. Worker source never writes that flag (`worker_state.py` still has a TODO). Readiness = fresh `workers.last_heartbeat` (within 60s) AND `metadata->>startup_phase = 'ready'`. Service-role key in the pod env is what makes heartbeats reach the RPC in the first place.
5. **User-queue preflight replaces per-task-level isolation**. Token `3HKcoLeJAFFfTFFeRV6Eu7Lq` is a dedicated test user (user confirmed, no production work). Before spawning/taking over, query `tasks` joined to `projects.user_id = <resolved_user>` for `status in ('Queued','In Progress') and coalesce(params->>'live_test','false') <> 'true'`. If any row, abort with a clear message. This also covers the PAT queue being user-wide / run_type-free — no need to fight the claim SQL.
6. **PAT-aware safety gate for Variant B takeover**. Replaces the `worker_id`-based check (PAT claims never stamp `tasks.worker_id`). New check: (a) any `tasks` row with `status='In Progress' AND generation_started_at > now() - interval '90 seconds'` for the target user, OR (b) `workers.last_heartbeat` for the pod is fresh (<60s). Either condition blocks takeover.
7. **Variant B branch/worktree capture**. Before any git ops, capture (a) the ORIGINAL branch name via `git symbolic-ref --short HEAD` (not just the SHA), (b) whether the working tree is dirty, (c) stash uncommitted changes with `git stash push --include-untracked -m "live-test-<ts>"`. Restore in reverse order on teardown. If HEAD was detached or already on a `live-test/*` branch, skip restore and log.
8. **RunPod defaults from `runpod/client.py`**, not `config.py`: `RUNPOD_GPU_TYPE`, `RUNPOD_WORKER_IMAGE`, `RUNPOD_TEMPLATE_ID`, storage volume / RAM tier constants — mirrored by the harness so fresh pods match production shape.
9. **Use existing fixtures where they exist**. Including `reigh-worker/artifacts/worker-matrix/20260316T141738351426Z/qwen_image_style_db_task/captured_generation_task.json`. Also consult `reigh-worker/scripts/create_test_task.py` and `reigh-worker/scripts/preview/fixtures.py` for travel task templates. LTX model name resolved by reading `reigh-worker/source/.../travel_guidance.py` / model registry in Stage 1 — not guessed.
10. **Variant B default `--no-terminate` TRUE** (we don't own the pod). Variant A default terminate-on-success.
11. **Continue-on-failure matrix** with per-type timeouts: image 15 min, `individual_travel_segment` 25 min, `travel_orchestrator` 40 min (all `--timeout-*` flag-configurable). One consolidated report at the end.

**Constraints:** additive only; no edits to orchestrator production paths or `reigh-worker/source/`; no force-push; no push to `main`; test token loaded only from env `REIGH_LIVE_TEST_TOKEN`.

## Phase 1: Prep / Finalize Unknowns (Read-Only)

### Step 1: Verify token schema + user-queue state (`reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/stage1_probe.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** a one-shot probe that (a) asserts `user_api_tokens` has plain `token` column (no `jti_hash`) via a targeted select, (b) resolves `REIGH_LIVE_TEST_TOKEN` to a single `user_id`, (c) dumps any `tasks` rows for that user with `status in ('Queued','In Progress')` and `params->>'live_test' IS DISTINCT FROM 'true'`. Prints findings; writes `scripts/live_test/stage1_findings.md` (gitignored).
2. **Run** the probe; confirm the test user has no stray work. If any rows appear, decide whether they are safe-to-ignore or need cleanup before proceeding.

### Step 2: Pin runtime constants (`reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/runpod/client.py`, `worker_startup.template.sh`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Read** `gpu_orchestrator/runpod/client.py` for `RUNPOD_GPU_TYPE`, `RUNPOD_WORKER_IMAGE`, `RUNPOD_TEMPLATE_ID`, storage volume + RAM tier constants. Mirror these defaults in `scripts/live_test/config.py` so `create_pod_and_wait(...)` calls match production shape.
2. **Read** `worker_startup.template.sh` around lines 167-183 and 355-366 for the apt deps + uv install sequence. Note which env vars (`SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`, `SUPABASE_URL`, `WORKER_DB_CLIENT_AUTH_MODE`, etc.) the template exports, because Variant A's custom bootstrap must export the same set plus `REIGH_ACCESS_TOKEN`.
3. **Read** `reigh-app/src/shared/components/SettingsModal/commandUtils.ts` to pin the exact `python run_worker.py ...` arg order and mirror it in the harness's launch-command builder.

### Step 3: Resolve models + pin fixture payloads
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Grep** `reigh-worker/source/` (especially `task_handlers/tasks/travel_guidance.py` and any model registry) for `ltx` / `LTX` to pick an actual supported LTX model identifier. Record in `stage1_findings.md`.
2. **Inventory** fixtures under `reigh-worker/artifacts/worker-matrix/`:
   - `20260316T135006000511Z/qwen_image_basic/captured_generation_task.json` → `qwen_image_t2i`
   - `.../qwen_image_edit_basic/captured_generation_task.json` → `qwen_image_edit`
   - `.../z_image_turbo_i2i_basic/captured_generation_task.json` → `z_image_turbo_i2i` (NOT bare `z_image_turbo`)
   - `20260316T141738351426Z/qwen_image_style_db_task/captured_generation_task.json` → `qwen_image_style` (this exists — do not hand-author)
   - `20260316T140127343314Z/wan22_i2v_individual_segment/captured_generation_task.json` → `individual_travel_segment`
3. **Consult** `reigh-worker/scripts/create_test_task.py` and `reigh-worker/scripts/preview/fixtures.py` for travel-orchestrator task templates. Clone + mutate these rather than hand-authoring for `travel_orchestrator_wan2_1seg` and `travel_orchestrator_ltx`. If no suitable template exists, fall back to reading the handler's param schema.
4. **Pick** 2 high-contrast anchor images with clear subjects. Bake their public URLs into `config.py` as defaults; matrix cases accept `--anchor-image-a` / `--anchor-image-b` overrides.
5. **Verify** `WGP_TASK_TYPES` at `reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/task_types.py:26-46` contains `z_image_turbo_i2i`, `qwen_image_style`, `qwen_image_2512`, etc.

**Deliverables:** `stage1_findings.md` (untracked) — schema state, resolved user_id, queue-clean assertion, RunPod constants (copied from `client.py`), LTX model id, fixture path map, anchor image URLs.
**Validation:** Probe runs clean; all fixtures load and parse; LTX model id is a real string in the source.

## Phase 2: Shared Primitives (`reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/`)

### Step 4: Package scaffold + config (`__init__.py`, `config.py`, `logger.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** `reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/` with package imports that add `reigh-worker-orchestrator/` to `sys.path` so `from gpu_orchestrator.database import DatabaseClient`, `from gpu_orchestrator.runpod.api import create_pod_and_wait, get_pod_ssh_details, terminate_pod`, and `from gpu_orchestrator.runpod.ssh import SSHClient` work directly. No copies of those primitives.
2. **`config.py`** loads from env: `REIGH_LIVE_TEST_TOKEN`, `RUNPOD_API_KEY`, `SUPABASE_URL`, `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`. Holds the RunPod defaults pinned in Step 2, fixture path map from Step 3, and default timeouts per task type (image=15m, individual_travel_segment=25m, travel_orchestrator=40m).

### Step 5: Token resolver (`token_resolver.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Implement** `resolve_token_to_user_id(db, token) -> str` via `db.supabase.table('user_api_tokens').select('user_id').eq('token', token).single().execute()`. Raise on zero/multiple rows. No SHA256 path (schema confirmed plain).

### Step 6: Preflight user-queue check (`preflight.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Implement** `assert_user_queue_clean(db, user_id)`: joins `tasks` to `projects` where `projects.user_id = ?` and filters `status in ('Queued','In Progress') and coalesce(params->>'live_test','false') <> 'true'`. Raises `UnexpectedUserWorkError` listing any offending task ids. Call from both variants before spawning/taking over.
2. **Implement** `get_or_create_live_test_project(db, user_id) -> project_id`: looks up a project with `user_id=? and name='live-test'`; inserts if missing.

### Step 7: Task spoofer (`task_spoofer.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** `load_fixture(case_name) -> dict` reading the JSON path from `config.FIXTURES` (Step 3).
2. **Implement** `insert_spoof_task(db, project_id, task_type, params_overrides) -> task_id`: clones fixture, merges overrides, sets `project_id`, `status='Queued'` (exact capitalization), stamps `params.live_test = True`, inserts via `db.supabase.table('tasks').insert(...)`. Mirrors the pattern in `reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/create_test_task.py:42-58`.

### Step 8: Completion poller (`completion_poller.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** `poll_until_complete(db, task_id, project_id, *, timeout_sec, interval_sec=5) -> TaskResult`. Polls `tasks` for `status in ('Complete','Failed','Cancelled')`. On terminal state, fetches `output_location` and queries `generations` for rows scoped to `project_id` within `(task.created_at, now)` (schema has no direct `task_id` FK).
2. **Returns** `TaskResult(task_id, case_name, task_type, final_status, output_location, generation_ids, elapsed_sec, error_summary)`. Never raises on task failure — records and returns.

### Step 9: Heartbeat waiter (`heartbeat_waiter.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Implement** `wait_until_ready(db, worker_id, *, timeout_sec=900, interval_sec=10)` that polls the `workers` table for: `last_heartbeat > now() - interval '60 seconds'` AND `metadata->>'startup_phase' = 'ready'`. Does NOT require `ready_for_tasks` (worker source never writes it per `worker_state.py` TODO).
2. **Rationale comment** in source citing the TODO so a future reader understands why the flag is omitted.

### Step 10: PAT-aware safety gate (`safety_gate.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Implement** `assert_safe_to_take_over(db, pod_id, user_id)` that raises `UnsafeTakeoverError` if ANY of:
   - Any `tasks` row with `status='In Progress' AND generation_started_at > now() - interval '90 seconds'` joined through `projects.user_id = user_id` (catches live PAT work — `worker_id` is NOT used since PAT claims don't stamp it).
   - `workers.last_heartbeat` for `worker_id=pod_id` is fresher than 60s (catches a pod whose supervisor is actively running, independent of claim mode).
2. **Docstring** explaining why we deliberately don't reuse `DatabaseClient.get_running_tasks_for_worker` — it filters on `tasks.worker_id`, which `claim_next_task_user_pat` (per `20250914000003_fix_pat_claim_function_outer_join.sql`) never populates.

### Step 11: Launch command builder (`launch_command.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Implement** `build_run_worker_command(workdir, *, reigh_token, supabase_url, worker_id, wgp_profile, idle_release_minutes) -> str` producing the canonical shell line:
   ```
   cd <workdir> && nohup uv run --python 3.10 --extra cuda124 \
     python run_worker.py \
       --supabase-url <supabase_url> \
       --reigh-access-token <token> \
       --worker <worker_id> \
       --wgp-profile <profile> \
       --idle-release-minutes <N> \
       --save-logging logs/worker.log \
     > logs/startup.log 2>&1 &
   ```
   Exactly mirrors `reigh-app/src/shared/components/SettingsModal/commandUtils.ts`. `run_worker.py` is the supervisor entrypoint — killing `worker.py` alone is insufficient because the supervisor respawns it (Variant B must kill both, see Step 12).
2. **Unit-test** that the constructed string contains `run_worker.py` and the exact flag set; `--idle-release-minutes 0` verified.

### Step 12: SSH bootstrap helpers (`ssh_bootstrap.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Thin wrapper** around `SSHClient` + `get_pod_ssh_details`:
   - `open_session(pod_id, api_key) -> SSHClient` — polls `get_pod_ssh_details` until SSH is up, then connects.
   - `clone_repo_into(ssh, workdir, repo_url, branch)` — `git clone --branch <branch> <url> <workdir>`.
   - `run_install(ssh, workdir)` — apt deps mirrored from `worker_startup.template.sh` + `cd <workdir> && uv sync --locked --extra cuda124`.
   - `export_env(env: dict) -> str` — builds the `export K=V` preamble used before `run_worker.py`. MUST include `REIGH_ACCESS_TOKEN`, `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`, `SUPABASE_URL`, `WORKER_DB_CLIENT_AUTH_MODE=worker`.
   - `capture_current_worker_cmdline(ssh) -> list[str] | None` — runs `ps -eo args | grep run_worker.py | grep -v grep | head -1` and returns the arg list (or None if no supervisor running). Used by Variant B to relaunch with original args on teardown.
   - `kill_supervisor_and_worker(ssh)` — `pkill -f run_worker.py; pkill -f 'source.runtime.worker'; sleep 2` + `pgrep` verification loop (up to 10s).
   - `launch_worker_detached(ssh, command_line)` — runs the string from `launch_command.build_run_worker_command`.
   - `fetch_worker_logs(ssh, workdir, lines=300)` — `tail -n <lines> logs/startup.log logs/worker.log`.

### Step 13: Terminate guard (`terminate_guard.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Implement** `guarded_terminate(pod_id, api_key, *, no_terminate: bool)` — skips `terminate_pod` if `no_terminate` or env `REIGH_LIVE_TEST_NO_TERMINATE=1`. Log the skip clearly. Driver wraps in try/finally so exception paths honor the opt-out.

### Step 14: Matrix runner + report (`matrix.py`, `report.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Define** `MATRIX` as a list of `MatrixCase(name, task_type, fixture_key, param_overrides, timeout_sec)`:
   - `travel_orchestrator_wan2_1seg` — template from Step 3, timeout 40m.
   - `travel_orchestrator_ltx` — template with LTX model id from Step 3, timeout 40m.
   - `individual_travel_segment` — fixture `wan22_i2v_individual_segment`, timeout 25m.
   - `qwen_image_style` — fixture `qwen_image_style_db_task`, style ref = anchor A, timeout 15m.
   - `qwen_image_t2i` — fixture `qwen_image_basic`, timeout 15m.
   - `qwen_image_2512` — mutation of `qwen_image_basic` with `task_type='qwen_image_2512'` + model override, timeout 15m.
   - `qwen_image_edit` — fixture `qwen_image_edit_basic`, input = anchor B, timeout 15m.
   - `z_image_turbo_i2i` — fixture `z_image_turbo_i2i_basic`, input = anchor B, timeout 15m. Uses the `_i2i` suffix task_type per `WGP_TASK_TYPES`.
2. **Implement** `run_matrix(db, project_id, cases) -> list[TaskResult]`: loops the cases, spoofs + polls each, CONTINUES on individual failures, returns the full list. Tasks can optionally be spoofed in parallel (flag `--serial` to force sequential for debugging).
3. **Implement** `write_report(results, variant, pod_id, out_dir)` producing `report.json` + `report.md` (per-task status, elapsed, output URLs, generation ids, error excerpts). Consolidated summary: `N/8 passed`.

### Step 15: Unit tests (`tests/test_primitives.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Pytest** with mocked `DatabaseClient` / `SSHClient`:
   - `test_token_resolver_returns_user_id` / `_raises_on_missing`.
   - `test_preflight_raises_on_stray_user_work` / `_passes_when_clean`.
   - `test_task_spoofer_stamps_live_test_and_queued_status`.
   - `test_completion_poller_returns_on_complete` / `_times_out` / `_records_failure`.
   - `test_safety_gate_rejects_fresh_in_progress_for_user` / `_rejects_fresh_heartbeat` / `_passes_when_stale_and_idle`.
   - `test_terminate_guard_respects_env_var` / `_respects_cli_flag` / `_skips_on_exception_path`.
   - `test_launch_command_uses_run_worker_py_and_idle_zero` — greps the built command string for `run_worker.py` and `--idle-release-minutes 0`.
   - `test_kill_supervisor_and_worker_targets_both_patterns` — verifies `pkill` argument strings.

**Deliverables:** ~13 primitive modules + unit tests.
**Validation:** `uv run pytest reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/tests/` green; existing orchestrator tests untouched.

## Phase 3: Variant Fresh Driver

### Step 16: Driver (`variant_fresh.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Flow** (called from `main.py` when `--variant fresh`):
   - `db = DatabaseClient()`; `user_id = resolve_token_to_user_id(db, token)`.
   - `assert_user_queue_clean(db, user_id)` — abort on stray work.
   - `project_id = get_or_create_live_test_project(db, user_id)`.
   - `pod = create_pod_and_wait(api_key, gpu_type_id=RUNPOD_GPU_TYPE, image_name=RUNPOD_WORKER_IMAGE, template_id=RUNPOD_TEMPLATE_ID, env_vars={'REIGH_ACCESS_TOKEN': token, 'SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY': service_key, 'SUPABASE_URL': supabase_url, 'WORKER_DB_CLIENT_AUTH_MODE': 'worker'}, name='reigh-live-test-fresh-<ts>', ...)`.
   - `ssh = open_session(pod_id, api_key)`.
   - `clone_repo_into(ssh, '/workspace/Reigh-Worker-LiveTest', 'https://github.com/banodoco/Reigh-Worker.git', branch=args.ref or 'main')`. Distinct dirname avoids collision with any Reigh-Worker dir that already exists.
   - `run_install(ssh, '/workspace/Reigh-Worker-LiveTest')`.
   - `cmd = build_run_worker_command('/workspace/Reigh-Worker-LiveTest', reigh_token=token, supabase_url=..., worker_id=pod_id, wgp_profile=3, idle_release_minutes=0)`.
   - `launch_worker_detached(ssh, export_env(env) + ' && ' + cmd)`.
   - `wait_until_ready(db, worker_id=pod_id, timeout_sec=900)`.
   - `results = run_matrix(db, project_id, MATRIX)`.
   - `write_report(results, variant='fresh', pod_id=pod_id, out_dir=...)`.
2. **Finally block** fetches worker/startup log excerpts via `fetch_worker_logs` into the report, then `guarded_terminate(pod_id, api_key, no_terminate=args.no_terminate)` (Variant Fresh default: terminate on success).

### Step 17: Dry-run for Variant Fresh
**Scope:** Small
1. **`--dry-run`** resolves token, runs preflight, loads all fixtures, prints the full planned launch command + task list, exits 0 without touching RunPod.

## Phase 4: Variant Update Driver

### Step 18: Git ops with branch + worktree capture (`git_ops.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** `snapshot_local_state(submodule_path='reigh-worker') -> LocalSnapshot`:
   - `original_branch = git -C <p> symbolic-ref --short HEAD` (None if detached).
   - `original_sha = git -C <p> rev-parse HEAD`.
   - `is_dirty = bool(git -C <p> status --porcelain)`.
   - If dirty: `git -C <p> stash push --include-untracked -m "live-test-<ts>"` and record the stash ref.
   - Returns `LocalSnapshot(original_branch, original_sha, stash_ref)`.
2. **Implement** `push_working_copy_to_temp_branch(submodule_path, snapshot) -> (branch_name, commit_sha)`:
   - If snapshot was dirty: `git stash apply <stash_ref>` to a fresh branch `live-test/<ts>-<shortsha>`, `git add -A`, `git commit -m "live-test: <ts>"`.
   - If clean: `git checkout -b live-test/<ts>-<shortsha>` from current HEAD (no commit needed).
   - `git push -u origin <branch>` (never force, never touches main).
3. **Implement** `restore_local_state(submodule_path, snapshot)`:
   - If `original_branch` is None (detached): `git checkout <original_sha>` and skip stash pop; log that branch context was lost.
   - Else: `git checkout <original_branch>`, `git reset --hard <original_sha>` only if no intermediate commits needed, and `git stash pop <stash_ref>` if present. Log each step.
4. **Implement** `cleanup_temp_branch(submodule_path, branch, *, preserve)`: deletes remote + local temp branch if `preserve=False`, else logs branch name prominently.

### Step 19: Variant Update driver (`variant_update.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **CLI modes** (via `main.py --variant update`):
   - `--pod-id <id>`: **B-existing** — takeover an existing Reigh-Worker pod.
   - `--spawn-takeover`: **B-fresh-takeover** — spawn a fresh pod via `create_pod_and_wait` with the production `worker_startup.template.sh` (stock env vars: `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`, template-default args), wait for heartbeat + `startup_phase='ready'`, then perform the takeover flow.
2. **Flow (both modes after pod is known):**
   - `db = DatabaseClient()`; `user_id = resolve_token_to_user_id(db, token)`.
   - `assert_user_queue_clean(db, user_id)`.
   - `assert_safe_to_take_over(db, pod_id, user_id)` — PAT-aware gate from Step 10.
   - `snapshot = snapshot_local_state('reigh-worker')`.
   - `branch, sha = push_working_copy_to_temp_branch('reigh-worker', snapshot)`.
   - `ssh = open_session(pod_id, api_key)`.
   - **Capture pod's original state:** `prev_remote_branch = ssh exec 'cd /workspace/Reigh-Worker && git symbolic-ref --short HEAD 2>/dev/null || echo DETACHED'`; `prev_remote_sha = ssh exec 'cd /workspace/Reigh-Worker && git rev-parse HEAD'`; `prev_cmdline = capture_current_worker_cmdline(ssh)`.
   - `cd /workspace/Reigh-Worker && git fetch origin && git checkout <branch> && git pull && uv sync --locked --extra cuda124`.
   - `kill_supervisor_and_worker(ssh)`.
   - Export env (including `REIGH_ACCESS_TOKEN` + `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` + `WORKER_DB_CLIENT_AUTH_MODE=worker`).
   - `launch_worker_detached(ssh, build_run_worker_command('/workspace/Reigh-Worker', reigh_token=token, worker_id=pod_id, wgp_profile=3, idle_release_minutes=0, ...))`.
   - `wait_until_ready(db, pod_id)`.
   - `results = run_matrix(db, project_id, MATRIX)`.
   - `write_report(results, variant='update', pod_id=pod_id, out_dir=...)`.
3. **Pod cleanup (success):**
   - If `prev_remote_branch == 'DETACHED'` or matches `live-test/*`: skip restore, log `"Pre-takeover pod state was scratch/detached; leaving worker stopped"`, do NOT relaunch.
   - Else: SSH `kill_supervisor_and_worker` (the test-run worker), `cd /workspace/Reigh-Worker && git checkout <prev_remote_branch> && git reset --hard <prev_remote_sha> && uv sync --locked --extra cuda124`, relaunch supervisor via the captured `prev_cmdline` (best-effort — if `prev_cmdline` is None, log and leave stopped).
   - `cleanup_temp_branch('reigh-worker', branch, preserve=False)`.
4. **Pod cleanup (failure):** leave the pod on the test branch (operator will inspect), `cleanup_temp_branch(..., preserve=True)`, log the branch name prominently.
5. **Always (finally):** `restore_local_state('reigh-worker', snapshot)` to put the dev machine submodule back as found. `guarded_terminate(pod_id, api_key, no_terminate=args.no_terminate)` — default `no_terminate=True` for Variant Update, only terminates if operator passes `--terminate`.

### Step 20: Single entrypoint (`main.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **CLI**: `python -m scripts.live_test.main --variant {fresh,update} [--pod-id ID | --spawn-takeover] [--no-terminate | --terminate] [--dry-run] [--ref BRANCH] [--wgp-profile N] [--timeout-image 900 --timeout-travel-segment 1500 --timeout-travel-orchestrator 2400] [--anchor-image-a URL --anchor-image-b URL] [--serial]`.
2. **Dispatches** to `variant_fresh.run(args)` or `variant_update.run(args)`.
3. **Defaults**: Variant fresh → `--terminate` (terminate on success). Variant update → `--no-terminate`. Both honor env `REIGH_LIVE_TEST_NO_TERMINATE=1`.

## Phase 5: Smoke + First Real Runs

### Step 21: Smoke script (`smoke.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Runs without touching GPUs:** (a) resolves token, (b) runs preflight user-queue check, (c) loads all 8 fixtures and validates JSON, (d) calls `build_run_worker_command(...)` and prints it for visual inspection, (e) optionally runs `assert_safe_to_take_over` against a `--pod-id` if provided, (f) exits 0/1. Used as the cheap CI gate.

### Step 22: First real run — Variant Fresh
**Scope:** Small (operational)
1. **Run** `python -m scripts.live_test.main --variant fresh --no-terminate` (keep pod for debugging if tasks fail). Iterate on any bugs until all 8 tasks complete. Promote to terminate-on-success once green.

### Step 23: First real run — Variant Update
**Scope:** Small (operational)
1. **Run** `python -m scripts.live_test.main --variant update --spawn-takeover` first (B-fresh-takeover) — never run Variant Update against a production pod on the first attempt.
2. **Only after** that passes, consider running B-existing against a real persistent pod, with operator confirmation.

---

## Phase 6: Cleanup

### Step 24: Gitignore + hygiene
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** to `reigh-worker/.gitignore`: `scripts/live_test/runs/`, `scripts/live_test/stage1_findings.md`, `scripts/live_test/.env*`.
2. **Verify** `git -C reigh-worker ls-remote origin 'refs/heads/live-test/*'` returns empty; delete any strays.
3. **Verify** test token not hardcoded anywhere: `grep -r '3HKcoLeJAFFfTFFeRV6Eu7Lq' reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/` returns zero hits.
4. **Verify** `reigh-worker/source/` and `reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/` are untouched via `git status`.

---

## Execution Order

1. **Phase 1** (read-only probes, constants, fixture inventory, LTX model id resolution) before any code.
2. **Phase 2** primitives + unit tests — fast feedback, catches schema/config drift before burning GPU minutes.
3. **Phase 3** Variant Fresh driver (lower blast radius — owned pod).
4. **Phase 4** Variant Update driver (higher blast radius — builds on Variant Fresh helpers).
5. **Phase 5** smoke → first real Variant Fresh run → first real Variant Update run (B-fresh-takeover only).
6. **Phase 6** cleanup after a green run.

## Validation Order

1. **Unit tests** (`uv run pytest reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/tests/`) — seconds, no network.
2. **Stage 1 probe** against real Supabase (read-only) — verifies token, schema, clean user queue.
3. **Smoke** (`python -m scripts.live_test.smoke --pod-id <any-test-pod>`) — no GPU, no inserts, no git pushes.
4. **Variant Fresh dry-run** — prints full plan + launch command.
5. **First real Variant Fresh run** with `--no-terminate`, iterate until 8/8 pass.
6. **Promote** Variant Fresh to terminate-on-success.
7. **Variant Update `--spawn-takeover`** against a freshly spawned pod, iterate until 8/8 pass.
8. **Existing orchestrator tests** (`uv run pytest reigh-worker-orchestrator/tests/`) still green.
9. **Existing worker tests** (`uv run pytest reigh-worker/tests/` if present) still green.

  "changes_summary": "Substantive architectural rework driven by user notes and verified gate flags. (1) Relocated harness to `reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/` and collapsed to a single `main.py --variant fresh|update` entrypoint with Variant B sub-modes B-existing / B-fresh-takeover. (2) Swapped the launch path from `worker.py` to the canonical `run_worker.py` supervisor entrypoint matching `commandUtils.ts`. (3) Corrected `--idle-release-minutes 0` semantics (it DISABLES idle release per `idle_release.py` \u2014 the prior plan's justification was backwards). (4) Added dual-credential injection: pod receives BOTH the PAT and `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` (plus `WORKER_DB_CLIENT_AUTH_MODE=worker`), because heartbeat RPCs are service-role-only while task claims stay on the PAT path. (5) Dropped `ready_for_tasks` from the readiness check (worker source never writes it per `worker_state.py` TODO); readiness = fresh heartbeat + `startup_phase='ready'`. (6) Replaced per-task isolation with a preflight user-queue check (`assert_user_queue_clean`) that aborts if the test user has any stray non-`live_test` Queued/In-Progress tasks \u2014 covers the user-wide, run_type-free PAT claim SQL. (7) Rewrote the Variant B safety gate to be PAT-aware: queries by user_id + `generation_started_at` window instead of `tasks.worker_id` (PAT claims never stamp worker_id). (8) Fixed workspace preservation: git ops now snapshot original branch name, dirty-state stash, and full restore order \u2014 not just commit SHA. (9) Pinned RunPod defaults from `runpod/client.py` (not `config.py`). (10) Added existing `qwen_image_style_db_task` fixture and directed Stage 1 to resolve the LTX model id from `travel_guidance.py` + use `reigh-worker/scripts/create_test_task.py` and `preview/fixtures.py` as travel templates. (11) Variant B defaults to `--no-terminate=True`; Variant A defaults to terminate-on-success. (12) Per-task-type configurable timeouts (image 15m, travel-segment 25m, travel-orchestrator 40m), continue-on-failure matrix, consolidated report. (13) Added `capture_current_worker_cmdline` so Variant B can relaunch the pod's original supervisor on teardown; skip-restore path for detached-HEAD or already-scratch branches. Added new success criteria covering preflight, dual-credential injection, `run_worker.py` path, PAT-aware gate, branch/stash capture, and dropped the now-outdated `get_running_tasks_for_worker`-based gate criterion.",

  "questions": [
    "Variant Update teardown relaunch: the plan captures `prev_cmdline` via `ps` before the takeover and uses it to relaunch the original supervisor on cleanup. If the pod was idle at takeover time (no supervisor running), `prev_cmdline` is None and the harness leaves the worker stopped. Is that acceptable, or should the harness fall back to launching the production `run_worker.py` with `--supabase-access-token $SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` + default profile to restore a working production state?",
    "Dirty-worktree stash behavior: if the user has uncommitted changes in `reigh-worker/` that can't cleanly `stash pop` after the test run (e.g., the remote temp branch introduced a conflict), should the harness leave the stash in place and print its ref for manual recovery, or attempt a best-effort merge?",
    "Service role key for Variant Update B-existing mode: we're injecting `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` into the pod env even for takeover \u2014 but the production pod's existing supervisor already has it. Is it OK to re-export it (harmless) or should the harness read the pod's existing env instead?",
    "B-fresh-takeover timing: after `create_pod_and_wait` with the production template, should the harness wait for the production worker to fully reach `startup_phase='ready'` before performing the takeover, or immediately take over after SSH is up (saves ~1-2 min but skips verifying the production startup actually works)?"

    "severity": "significant"

The repo's single centralized builder emits `python run_worker.py --reigh-access-token ... --wgp-profile ... --idle-release-minutes ...`, which goes through the supervisor entrypoint, but the plan's Variant A command switches to `python worker.py ...`; that diverges from the requested buildWorkerLaunchLine-equivalent path and from the plan's own Variant B assumption that supervisor must be killed and relaunched.",

PAT claims are scoped to the user but not to a project or run_type, so creating a dedicated `live-test` project only prevents other users' workers from taking the test tasks; it does not prevent the harness worker from consuming the target user's real queued tasks, which means the plan only partially satisfies the 'must not interfere with production runs' requirement.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"

That means the plan both overrides an explicit requirement and justifies the override with the opposite of the repo's actual behavior.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },
  {
    "id": "correctness-1",
    "concern": "Are the proposed changes technically correct?: Checked the worker heartbeat path in reigh-worker/source/runtime/worker/server.py and reigh-worker/source/runtime/worker/guardian.py against the grants in reigh-app/supabase/migrations/20260311110000_restrict_worker_heartbeat_rpc_grants.sql.     "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"

      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-008",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-018",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-106",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-107",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "did-the-work-fully-address-the-issue-hints-user-notes-and-approved-plan-requirements": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-013",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the package is internally inconsistent. the plan body says to keep `activepairdata` in `usestate` and make phase 2 optional, but the attached metadata and success criteria still describe the previous derive-via-`usememo` / remove-`setactivepairdata` / simplify-`onpairclick` plan. that means the approved-plan requirements are only partially coherent as presented.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-108",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the linux distro note is documentation-only; the generated install command in step 7 still runs `apt-get install python3.10-venv python3.10-dev ffmpeg` without any pre-check that the package exists. users on ubuntu 24.04+ who ignore the doc and try to copy-paste the command will hit a confusing `e: unable to locate package python3.10-venv` from apt rather than a targeted error from commandutils.ts. a one-line detection pre-check (e.g., `apt-cache show python3.10-venv >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo 'install deadsnakes ppa first \u2014 see readme'; exit 1; }`) would convert the silent failure into an actionable error, but the plan does not add this.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-014",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: phase 1 still misses a real supporting-data location: `trailingpairdata` lives outside `pairdatabyindex` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts#l111), but the proposed sync logic only considers `pairdatabyindex`. if trailing slot regenerate is part of the bug surface, that location is not covered.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-110",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the non-swallowing helper `update_worker_phase_strict` is a new shell function, but phase 1 step 2 introduces it only as pseudo-code. there is no concrete note that the helper must handle supabase http non-2xx responses as failures (curl returns 0 even on http 4xx/5xx by default unless `-f` or `--fail-with-body` is used). if the implementer ports `update_worker_phase` verbatim with only `|| return 0` removed, a 500 from supabase would still report success because curl's exit code is 0. the plan should specify `curl --fail` or explicit http status code checking in the strict variant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-109",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }
  ],

    "subsystem": "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct",
    "total_occurrences": 5,
    "plan_count": 2,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-007",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-008",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-018",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-106",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-107",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      }
    ]

Read this file first — it contains 5 checks, each with a question and guidance.
For each check, investigate the codebase, then add your findings to the `findings` array for that check.

Each finding needs:
- "detail": what you specifically checked and what you found (at least a full sentence)
- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.
- Every check must end with at least one finding. Never leave a `findings` array empty. If you found no issue, add one detailed `flagged: false` finding explaining what you checked and why it appears clear.

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
Bad: {"detail": "No issue found", "flagged": false}  ← too brief, will be rejected
Bad: {"detail": "The hints suggest approach X but the plan uses Y. However Y is consistent with X's intent.", "flagged": false}  ← a different approach than the hints IS a flag. You found a divergence — flag it. The gate decides if it's acceptable.

After filling in checks, add any additional concerns to the `flags` array (e.g., security, performance, dependencies).
Use the standard format (id, concern, category, severity_hint, evidence). This array can be empty.

Workflow: read the file → investigate → read file again → add finding → write file back. Repeat for each check.

This is critique iteration 2. The template file includes prior findings with their status. Verify addressed flags were actually fixed, re-flag if inadequate, and check for new issues introduced by the revision.

        Additional guidelines:
        - Robustness level: standard. Use balanced judgment. Flag significant risks, but do not spend flags on minor polish or executor-obvious boilerplate.
        - Over-engineering: prefer the simplest approach that fully solves the problem.
        - Reuse existing flag IDs when the same concern is still open.
        - `verified_flag_ids`: list flag IDs from prior iterations that the revised plan actually resolves (e.g., if the plan was revised to fix FLAG-001, and you confirm the fix is correct, include "FLAG-001"). Only include flags you've verified — don't guess.
        - Verify that the plan follows the expected structure when validator warnings or the outline suggest drift.
        - Additional flags may use these categories: correctness, security, completeness, performance, maintainability, other.
        - Focus on concrete issues, not structural formatting.

Verified prior `FLAG-001` through `FLAG-005` as addressed. Added four new concerns:

- `FLAG-006`: Variant Fresh waits on `startup_phase='ready'`, but only the orchestrator startup template writes that metadata.
- `FLAG-007`: `B-fresh-takeover` relies on `create_pod_and_wait()` to run the stock template, but provisioning and template launch are separate steps in the repo.
- `FLAG-008`: takeover/restore helpers only model `run_worker.py`, while production-template pods run `worker.py` directly.
- `FLAG-009`: `B-fresh-takeover` self-blocks because it waits for a fresh heartbeat and then rejects fresh heartbeats in the safety gate.

        Engineering brief produced from the codebase and task details:

        ### Task Summary
        Design and implement a live end-to-end test harness for the Reigh platform that exercises the individual-user worker code path (not orchestrator-managed scaling) against real RunPod GPUs and real Supabase tasks, scoped to the user who owns Reigh access [REDACTED] Deliver two driver variants (A: fresh pod + fresh clone into a non-default directory name; B: update an existing Reigh-Worker pod from a local working copy pushed to a temp branch), both running the same 8-task matrix, both verifying generations rows, with reusable primitives (token-to-user resolver, task spoofer, poll-until-complete, SSH/pod helpers borrowed from orchestrator).

        ### Key Evidence
        - Orchestrator's existing startup template hardcodes --supabase-access-token "$SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY" and --wgp-profile 1; does NOT pass --reigh-access-token or --idle-release-minutes. 'buildWorkerLaunchLine-equivalent' in the task means we must build a new launch command from scratch — cannot reuse render_startup_script() as-is for either variant. (source: reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/runpod/worker_startup.template.sh:355-366; startup_script.py:38-65; relevance: high)
- The worker DOES accept --reigh-access-token (server.py:226-228), --idle-release-minutes (parsed via idle_release.add_cli_args line 229, default 15.0, triggers exit code 143 when idle queue polling exceeds the duration), and --wgp-profile (int, 1-5). (source: reigh-worker/source/runtime/worker/server.py:211-245; source/runtime/worker/idle_release.py; relevance: high)
- terminate_pod(pod_id, api_key) has NO opt-out flag in current code — it is a bare wrapper around runpod.terminate_pod(). The task's 'opt-out flag' requirement means the harness needs its own guard (CLI flag --no-terminate or env var REIGH_LIVE_TEST_NO_TERMINATE) that the driver honors. (source: reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/runpod/api.py:223-229; relevance: high)
- SCHEMA DISCREPANCY: task says 'via user_api_tokens.jti_hash', but migration 20250713000008 DROPPED jti_hash and expires_at. Current schema is (id, user_id, token [plain text, unique], label). Token resolution is: select user_id from user_api_tokens where [REDACTED]. No SHA256 hashing. MUST be verified in stage 1 before building the resolver. (source: reigh-app/supabase/migrations/20250711000001_create_user_api_tokens.sql; 20250713000008_*.sql; functions/generate-pat/index.ts:9-14; relevance: high)
- Tasks are scoped to a user indirectly via projects: tasks.project_id -> projects.id -> projects.user_id. There is NO tasks.user_id column. To 'spoof tasks scoped to the user the token resolves to' the harness must: (a) resolve token->user_id, (b) find or create a project owned by that user, (c) insert task with that project_id. Task status enum values EXACT: 'Queued','In Progress','Complete','Failed','Cancelled'. (source: reigh-app/supabase/migrations/20250100000000_create_base_schema.sql:4,25-37; relevance: high)
- Task claim path: worker POSTs {SUPABASE_URL}/functions/v1/claim-next-task with {worker_id, run_type:'gpu', same_model_only:true}. Edge function handles Queued->In Progress. Completion via complete-task / update-task-status edge functions which also write to generations. 'Verify generation was produced' means polling tasks.status='Complete' AND querying generations table by project_id + timestamp window (no direct task_id FK on generations). (source: reigh-worker/source/core/db/task_claim.py:163-257; reigh-app/supabase/functions/complete-task/generation-handlers.ts; relevance: high)
- SSH helper already built: gpu_orchestrator.runpod.ssh.SSHClient (paramiko), authenticates via private key env var > private key file > password. DatabaseClient.execute_command_on_worker(runpod_id, command, timeout) wraps it with pod SSH details lookup. Both directly reusable. (source: reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/runpod/ssh.py:11-107; client.py:175-203; relevance: high)
- Heartbeat detection: worker POSTs /rest/v1/rpc/func_update_worker_heartbeat (or func_worker_heartbeat_with_logs) every ~20s updating workers.last_heartbeat. 'Ready' = (a) workers.last_heartbeat > created_at + 5s, (b) metadata.startup_phase='ready', (c) metadata.ready_for_tasks=true. Poll workers table by worker_id (pod_id) for fresh heartbeat. (source: reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/worker_state.py:150-170; database.py:299-315; reigh-worker/source/runtime/worker/guardian.py:130-154; relevance: high)
- Workdir discovery in template.sh is hardcoded to $WORKSPACE_DIR/Reigh-Worker (primary) or Headless-Wan2GP (fallback). For Variant A's 'NEW name Reigh-Worker-LiveTest' requirement, must write a custom startup script (or run install via SSHClient.execute_command) that clones into the custom directory. Cannot rely on the existing template or build_launch_command(). (source: reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/runpod/worker_startup.template.sh:167-183; startup_script.py:12-25; relevance: high)
- Variant B 'in-flight task' safety check: reject takeover if workers.metadata.ready_for_tasks=true and any tasks row has worker_id=<pod_worker_id> with status='In Progress', OR workers.last_heartbeat fresh (<60s). Use DatabaseClient.get_running_tasks_for_worker(worker_id) which already implements this. (source: reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/database.py:346-366; relevance: high)
- Reference test payloads already exist at reigh-worker/artifacts/worker-matrix/*/captured_generation_task.json — qwen_image_basic, z_image_turbo_basic, wan22_i2v_individual_segment and others. These are the authoritative fixtures for the task spoofer's payload shapes. Read and mutate them rather than hand-authoring. (source: reigh-worker/artifacts/worker-matrix/20260316T135006000511Z/*, 20260316T140127343314Z/*; relevance: high)
- WGP_TASK_TYPES frozenset enumerates exact worker-recognized task_type strings: 'qwen_image','qwen_image_edit','qwen_image_style','qwen_image_2512','z_image_turbo','z_image_turbo_i2i' (I2I variant suffix). 'travel_orchestrator' and 'individual_travel_segment' are orchestrator task types handled separately. Task says 'z_image_turbo I2I' which maps to z_image_turbo_i2i. (source: reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/task_types.py:26-46; relevance: high)
- Existing reusable scaffold: reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/create_test_task.py already inserts {task_type, project_id, params, status:'Queued'} via DatabaseClient.supabase.table('tasks').insert(...). Task spoofer should extend this pattern. (source: reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/create_test_task.py:18-74; relevance: medium)
- Prior plan 'build-a-spoofable-end-to-end-20260410-1456' and 'look-at-how-we-create-tasks-20260410-1842' exist in .megaplan/plans/. Worth skimming in stage 1 for reusable primitives or decisions. (source: .megaplan/plans/build-a-spoofable-end-to-end-20260410-1456/; .megaplan/plans/look-at-how-we-create-tasks-20260410-1842/; relevance: medium)
- Worker supervisor restarts on idle exit code 143 (supervisor.py:1-81). For Variant B 'cleanly restart worker': killing worker.py alone is NOT enough — supervisor will resurrect it with OLD CLI args. Harness must kill supervisor too, then launch a fresh supervisor with new --reigh-access-token flag. (source: reigh-worker/source/runtime/supervisor.py:1-81; relevance: high)
- Variant B pushes local working copy to banodoco/Reigh-Worker on a temp branch. reigh-worker is a git submodule of the workspace. Requires: (a) valid git credentials on dev machine, (b) commit of uncommitted local changes to the temp branch, (c) cleanup deletes temp branch post-run. Force-push never needed. Must NOT modify main. (source: reigh-worker/ submodule; remote banodoco/Reigh-Worker.git per worker_startup.template.sh:181; relevance: high)
