        ### Test Expectations
        - [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::variant_a::fresh_install: create_pod_and_wait, SSH in, clone banodoco/Reigh-Worker into /workspace/Reigh-Worker-LiveTest, run install (apt + uv sync --locked --extra cuda124), launch worker.py with [REDACTED] --wgp-profile 3 --idle-release-minutes 0, poll workers table until fresh heartbeat + metadata.startup_phase='ready'. Run 8-task matrix via task spoofer, poll tasks until Complete, assert generations rows exist for project_id within time window. Report per-task pass/fail with output_location URL. Finally terminate_pod unless --no-terminate set.
- [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::variant_b::update_existing: Target existing pod with /workspace/Reigh-Worker/ (or spawn new + standard install + take over). Safety check rejects if any tasks In Progress for pod worker OR workers.last_heartbeat < 60s. Commit local reigh-worker working copy to temp branch live-test/<ts>-<hash> on banodoco/Reigh-Worker, push. SSH in, git fetch + checkout temp branch + pull, uv sync --locked --extra cuda124. Kill supervisor+worker, re-launch with --reigh-access-token. Wait for heartbeat. Run same 8-task matrix, assert Complete + generations. On success, restore pod to previous branch/commit and delete temp branch. On failure, leave branch for debugging.
- [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::task_matrix::travel_orchestrator_wan2_1seg: Inserts travel_orchestrator task with 1-segment Wan 2.2 i2v payload; verifies orchestrator decomposes into individual_travel_segment, segment completes, generations row written.
- [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::task_matrix::travel_orchestrator_ltx: Same as above but with LTX model variant in segment params.
- [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::task_matrix::individual_travel_segment: Direct individual_travel_segment task (bypassing orchestrator); payload cloned from artifacts/worker-matrix/wan22_i2v_individual_segment fixture.
- [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::task_matrix::qwen_image_style: qwen_image_style task with a style reference image URL; verifies worker downloads ref, runs style transfer, writes output.
- [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::task_matrix::qwen_image_t2i: qwen_image text-to-image task; payload cloned from qwen_image_basic fixture.
- [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::task_matrix::qwen_image_2512: qwen_image_2512 task variant (newer model checkpoint).
- [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::task_matrix::qwen_image_edit: qwen_image_edit with input image; verifies edit pipeline writes generation.
- [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::task_matrix::z_image_turbo_i2i: z_image_turbo_i2i (I2I variant with _i2i suffix, NOT bare z_image_turbo) with input image; payload derived from z_image_turbo_basic fixture mutated to i2i shape.
- [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::primitives::token_user_resolver: Given [REDACTED] returns the correct user_id. Verifies schema state matches (plain token column, not jti_hash).
- [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::primitives::variant_b_safety_gate: Given pod with In Progress task OR fresh heartbeat, Variant B driver refuses to take over and exits non-zero. Unit-testable with mock DatabaseClient.
- [fail_to_pass] live_e2e::primitives::terminate_opt_out: With REIGH_LIVE_TEST_NO_TERMINATE=1 or --no-terminate, driver completes and does NOT call terminate_pod (verifiable by mock).
- [pass_to_pass] existing::tests/gpu_orchestrator/runpod/test_startup_script.py: Existing unit tests for render_startup_script / build_launch_command. Must continue to pass — harness adds NEW builders without mutating existing ones.
- [pass_to_pass] existing::reigh-worker-orchestrator tests: Unit tests for DatabaseClient, SSHClient, lifecycle — harness must not modify shared primitives in breaking ways.

        ### Constraints
        - MUST NOT modify the orchestrator's existing worker_startup.template.sh, render_startup_script, or any production lifecycle code path. Harness is additive — new files under reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/.
- MUST NOT interfere with production orchestrator runs. Spoofed tasks MUST be scoped to the test user ([REDACTED]). Stage 1 must confirm how task routing works — if pool-wide, use a dedicated live-test project and a params.live_test=true marker so a production worker claiming a test task is harmless.
- MUST NOT force-push or push to main. Variant B pushes to temp branch live-test/<timestamp>-<short-hash> and deletes on success. Never --no-verify, never amend shared commits.
- MUST NOT clobber pods doing real user work. Variant B safety gate is mandatory: reject takeover if pod has any tasks In Progress for worker_id, or fresh (<60s) heartbeat.
- MUST include an opt-out flag (env REIGH_LIVE_TEST_NO_TERMINATE=1 and/or --no-terminate CLI flag) that skips terminate_pod() on both normal-exit and exception paths.
- MUST resolve token->user via the CURRENT schema (plain `token` column). If stage 1 discovers production still uses jti_hash, fall back to SHA256(token) hex. Do not guess — verify first.
- MUST use exact task_status enum capitalization: 'Queued', 'In Progress', 'Complete', 'Failed', 'Cancelled'.
- MUST use z_image_turbo_i2i (with _i2i suffix) for the I2I matrix entry, not z_image_turbo.
- Task matrix failures reported per-task (8 rows), not all-or-nothing. 7/8 passing still produces a useful report.
- Per-task completion polling must have a configurable timeout (suggest 20 min default) and capture final status + tasks.output_location + generations.id list within project scope + time window.
- Variant B post-run cleanup: restore pod to previous branch/commit via git checkout <prev>, THEN delete temp remote branch. On failure, preserve temp branch and print its name.
- Secrets (RUNPOD_API_KEY, SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY, SSH private key) loaded from existing .env locations (reigh-worker-orchestrator/.env) — never hardcoded or prompted for.

        ### Suggested Approach
        Stage 1 (prep/finalize unknowns): (a) Verify user_api_tokens schema state in production by reading migrations in order and running a read-only schema probe via DatabaseClient — confirm plain token column vs jti_hash. (b) Confirm how individual-user worker routing scopes task claims — is there a user/worker identity filter in claim-next-task, or is the queue pool-wide? If pool-wide, create a dedicated 'live-test' project under the target user and tag tasks with params.live_test=true so any stray claim by a real worker is harmless. (c) Skim .megaplan/plans/build-a-spoofable-end-to-end-20260410-1456/ and look-at-how-we-create-tasks-20260410-1842/ for reusable primitives. (d) Confirm RunPod GPU type + template_id (read orchestrator config defaults in config.py). (e) Pin the 8 payload shapes by reading artifacts/worker-matrix/* + qwen_image_style/qwen_image_2512/qwen_image_edit handler param expectations. Deliverables: stage1_findings.md. Files touched: none (read-only). Risks: production schema may differ from checked-in migrations. Validation: schema probe returns expected columns; routing confirmed from claim-next-task edge source.

Stage 2 (shared primitives): Create reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/ package: (i) token_resolver.py — token->user_id using confirmed schema. (ii) project_for_user.py — get-or-create a dedicated live-test project. (iii) task_spoofer.py — insert_task(task_type, params, project_id) loading reference payloads from artifacts/worker-matrix/ and mutating per test. (iv) completion_poller.py — poll_until_complete(task_id, timeout_sec) returns (final_status, output_location, generation_ids) via tasks + generations tables. (v) ssh_bootstrap.py — wraps SSHClient with clone_repo_into(dir, url, branch), run_uv_sync(workdir), kill_supervisor_and_worker(), launch_worker(workdir, cli_args, worker_id, env). (vi) heartbeat_waiter.py — poll workers table until fresh heartbeat + phase=ready. (vii) terminate_guard.py — honors env/CLI opt-out. Deliverables: 7 primitive modules + unit tests with mocked DatabaseClient/SSHClient. Files touched: new under scripts/live_test/. Risks: drift from orchestrator helper semantics. Validation: unit tests pass; ruff/mypy clean.

Stage 3 (Variant A driver — fresh install): variant_a.py calls create_pod_and_wait (reusing orchestrator GPU config + template + env_vars, injecting REIGH_ACCESS_TOKEN env var), waits for SSH, runs apt deps + uv installer + `git clone https://github.com/banodoco/Reigh-Worker.git /workspace/Reigh-Worker-LiveTest`, cd, `uv sync --locked --extra cuda124`, then launches worker via `nohup uv run --python 3.10 --extra cuda124 python worker.py --supabase-url <url> [REDACTED] --worker <pod_id> --wgp-profile 3 --idle-release-minutes 0 > logs/startup.log 2>&1 &`. Calls heartbeat_waiter, then runs the 8-task matrix via task_spoofer + completion_poller, aggregates results, prints report, calls terminate_guard. Deliverables: variant_a.py + CLI entry. Risks: RunPod timeouts; apt mirror issues; uv network hiccups. Validation: dry-run passes; first real run with --no-terminate succeeds on at least qwen_image (fast task).

Stage 4 (Variant B driver — update existing): variant_b.py takes --pod-id (existing) or spawns + does standard install first. Runs safety_gate. Commits local reigh-worker tree: `git -C reigh-worker checkout -b live-test/<ts>-<sha>`, `git add`, `git commit`, `git push -u origin <branch>`. SSH in, `cd /workspace/Reigh-Worker && git fetch origin && PREV_REF=$(git rev-parse HEAD) && git checkout <branch> && git pull && uv sync --locked --extra cuda124`. kill_supervisor_and_worker, re-launch with --reigh-access-token. heartbeat_waiter. Same 8-task matrix + reporting. On cleanup: SSH `git checkout $PREV_REF`, delete temp remote branch. On failure: preserve temp branch, print name. Deliverables: variant_b.py. Risks: submodule git state; credentials; supervisor races; clobbering real work. Validation: safety gate unit test; first real run on freshly-spawned test pod, not a production pod.

Stage 5 (dry-run/smoke-test harness itself): Before touching a real GPU: unit-test pass on primitives with mocked DatabaseClient + SSHClient. Add --dry-run flag to both variants that (a) resolves token, (b) runs Variant B safety gate against a real pod, (c) loads every payload fixture and validates shape against handler params, (d) does NOT spawn pods or push branches. Catches schema drift and missing secrets without burning GPU minutes. Deliverables: --dry-run on both drivers + scripts/live_test/smoke.py. Validation: smoke prints expected plan for both variants.

Stage 6 (first real run): Run Variant A first (lower blast radius). Use --no-terminate on first attempt so failed pods stay up for SSH debugging. Iterate until all 8 tasks pass, then promote to terminate-on-success. Then Variant B against a freshly-spawned-and-standard-installed pod (not a production pod). Only use reused persistent-volume pods once the driver is proven. Deliverables: two successful run reports (JSON + log) in .megaplan/plans/<slug>/runs/. Risks: real worker bugs surfaced (good!), test infra bugs (iterate). Validation: report shows 8/8 pass.

Stage 7 (cleanup/documentation): Purge lingering temp branches on banodoco/Reigh-Worker (`git ls-remote | grep live-test/` should be empty). Update reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/README or a new docs/live_test.md ONLY if user requests docs — per project policy, do not proactively create docs. Add .gitignore entry for report output directory. NEVER check in the test token — load from env or untracked local file. Validation: stale branches purged; .gitignore updated.

File layout: reigh-worker-orchestrator/scripts/live_test/ (alongside existing create_test_task.py, test_supabase.py) — naturally shares DatabaseClient + runpod primitives without cross-repo imports. Keep reigh-worker itself untouched — no worker-side changes needed.

Top risks: (1) Task routing: if tasks aren't actually scoped per-user, test tasks could be stolen by real workers or vice versa — stage 1 MUST resolve this before stage 2 work starts. (2) --idle-release-minutes 0 may cause the worker to exit before claiming the first task if queue polling is slow — consider 1 minute with a bumped idle_onboarding_grace_seconds. (3) Supervisor restart: killing worker.py alone causes relaunch with OLD args — must kill supervisor first. (4) Variant B git push requires credentials on the dev machine. (5) terminate_pod has no real opt-out in orchestrator code, so the flag must be enforced in the harness's own code path — honor it on both normal-exit AND exception paths.

        The engineering brief above was produced by analyzing the codebase. Use it as a strong starting point, but the suggested approach is a hypothesis — verify it's the best fix, not just a valid one.

        Idea:
Design a robust end-to-end live test harness for the Reigh platform that exercises the individual-user worker path (NOT the orchestrator-managed worker path) against a specific user identified by Reigh access [REDACTED] Two variants of one live test, both targeting a real RunPod GPU, both creating real spoof tasks in Supabase for that specific user, both waiting for actual task completion and verifying generations were produced end-to-end. Variant A 'Fresh Install': spin up a new RunPod pod using the orchestrator's create_pod_and_wait approach, SSH in and clone reigh-worker into a folder with a NEW name (NOT Reigh-Worker, e.g. Reigh-Worker-LiveTest), run buildWorkerLaunchLine-equivalent install/launch with [REDACTED] --wgp-profile 3 --idle-release-minutes 0, wait for heartbeat, insert spoof tasks in Supabase scoped to the user that the token resolves to (via user_api_tokens.jti_hash), poll until each task reaches Complete with generations row, report pass/fail per task with output URLs, terminate pod via terminate_pod() with opt-out flag. Variant B 'Update Existing': target an existing pod with /workspace/Reigh-Worker/ installed (or spawn+standard-install then take over), push current local working copy of reigh-worker/ to a temporary branch on banodoco/Reigh-Worker, SSH in, git fetch+checkout+pull, uv sync --locked, cleanly restart worker with --reigh-access-token for test user, run same task matrix, on success optionally delete temp branch and restore pod to previous branch/state. Variant B must not clobber pods doing real user work — include safety check for in-flight tasks. Task matrix (one of each): travel_orchestrator standard Wan 2 images 1 segment, travel_orchestrator LTX variant, individual_travel_segment single segment, qwen_image_style with style ref, qwen_image text-to-image, qwen_image_2512, qwen_image_edit, z_image_turbo I2I. Stages: (1) prep/finalize unknowns, (2) build shared primitives (token→user resolver, task spoofer, poll-until-complete, SSH helpers reused from orchestrator), (3) build Variant A driver, (4) build Variant B driver, (5) dry-run/smoke-test the harness itself, (6) first real run, (7) cleanup/documentation. For each stage: concrete deliverables, files to touch, risks, validation.

User notes and answers:
- User decisions on blockers and open questions (authoritative — supersedes earlier plan choices):

BLOCKER 1 (isolation): [REDACTED] is ALREADY a dedicated test user account. It has no production work and is explicitly a test token. User confirmed we can proceed using this token directly. Still do a defensive preflight check (abort if any unexpected non-test Queued/In-Progress task exists for the user, as a safety net), but no need to pause production, create a new test user, or use service-role for claims.

BLOCKER 2 (readiness signal): Option (a). Inject BOTH the PAT ([REDACTED]) AND SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY into the pod. The worker must still run the INDIVIDUAL-USER PAT path for task claims (WORKER_DB_CLIENT_AUTH_MODE=worker) — this is a hard requirement. The service role key is injected purely so heartbeat RPCs work and the standard ready_for_tasks detection path is available. Do NOT switch to service-role claim path.

BLOCKER 3 (Variant B takeover safety gate): Orchestrator's call — use option (a). Implement a PAT-aware in-flight check that queries tasks for the target user filtered by status (In Progress) and generation_started_at (recent window) to detect any live PAT work on the pod before takeover. Do NOT rely on the worker_id column (PAT claims don't stamp it).

OPEN QUESTIONS (all answered):
- Q1 driver location: reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/ (INSIDE reigh-worker repo, NOT reigh-worker-orchestrator). The driver imports SSH / runpod primitives FROM reigh-worker-orchestrator but lives in the worker repo.
- Q2 credentials: env vars RUNPOD_API_KEY, SUPABASE_URL, SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY, REIGH_LIVE_TEST_TOKEN (never committed, loaded from .env or shell env).
- Q4 LTX model: resolve from reigh-worker source (travel_guidance.py / model registry) in Stage 1. Pick an actual supported LTX model name by reading the code, not guessing.
- Q5 individual_travel_segment standalone: existing handler already supports it as part of the travel flow. Use it directly — no synthetic parent needed.
- Q6 timeouts: longer to absorb model/asset downloads on fresh pods. Default travel_orchestrator 40 min, individual_travel_segment 25 min, image tasks 15 min. All configurable via flag.
- Q7 failure handling: run ALL tasks in the matrix regardless of individual failures. Emit one consolidated pass/fail report at the end showing per-task status, output URLs, durations, and error summaries.
- Q8 fixtures: pick good anchor images — 2 high-contrast scenes with clear subjects. Bake them into the driver as the default set, but make them swappable via config.

DEFAULTS (confirmed, not overridden):
- Flag 4 launch path: use run_worker.py via the supervisor (the canonical buildWorkerLaunchLine path from reigh-app/src/shared/components/SettingsModal/commandUtils.ts). Do NOT shell out to worker.py directly. Variant B worker restarts must kill BOTH supervisor.py AND its child worker process, then relaunch via run_worker.py.
- Flag 5 idle release: use --idle-release-minutes 0 which disables idle release (<=0 disables per source/runtime/worker/idle_release.py:41-43). Correct default.

VARIANT B TEARDOWN (B1/B2/B3):
- B1 pod restore: After the test finishes (pass or fail), restore the pod to its pre-takeover state — git checkout the original branch (captured at start of takeover) and relaunch the original supervisor+worker process. EXCEPTION: if the pre-takeover branch was itself a scratch/throwaway branch (e.g., another live-test/* branch or a detached HEAD), skip restoration and leave the worker stopped.
- B2 termination: Variant B defaults to --no-terminate TRUE. We do not own the pod, so never terminate by default; operator must explicitly opt in with a flag if they want termination.
- B3 Variant B sub-modes: Variant B supports TWO operating modes and must implement both:
  * B-existing: operator passes in an existing pod ID; driver assumes a Reigh-Worker install is already present at /workspace/Reigh-Worker.
  * B-fresh-takeover: driver spawns a fresh pod using create_pod_and_wait(), lets the standard production orchestrator startup template run (install + worker start), waits for the standard install to complete and the worker to come online, THEN performs the branch push + git pull + worker restart takeover. Same task matrix runs after takeover in both modes.

GLOBAL NOTES:
- Variant A folder name: use Reigh-Worker-LiveTest (or similar distinct name) at /workspace to avoid collision with any existing Reigh-Worker install.
- The plan must treat the individual-user PAT path as the PRIMARY worker auth mode for all test runs. Service role key is only present to unblock heartbeat writes and metadata updates, never for task claim calls.
- The driver is a single Python entrypoint with --variant fresh|update and mode flags, reusing SSH/pod primitives from reigh-worker-orchestrator.

        Plan:
        # Implementation Plan: Live E2E Test Harness for Individual-User Worker Path

## Overview

Build an additive live test harness at `reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/` (inside the reigh-worker repo, importing primitives from the sibling `reigh-worker-orchestrator` package) that exercises the individual-user PAT worker code path against a real RunPod GPU and real Supabase, scoped to the dedicated test-user token `3HKcoLeJAFFfTFFeRV6Eu7Lq`. One Python entrypoint with `--variant fresh|update` and Variant B sub-modes.

**Settled decisions (do not re-open):** `run_worker.py` is the canonical local launch path; `--idle-release-minutes 0` disables idle release (intended); dual credential injection (PAT + service-role) with `WORKER_DB_CLIENT_AUTH_MODE=worker`; preflight `assert_user_queue_clean` is the isolation story; PAT-aware safety gate keyed on `generation_started_at`; harness package lives inside `reigh-worker/`; Variant Update default `--no-terminate`; continue-on-failure matrix with per-type timeouts (15m/25m/40m).

**V3 architectural corrections (addressing v2 gate flags):**

1. **Readiness waiter drops `startup_phase='ready'` entirely.** Only `worker_startup.template.sh` writes that metadata — no code under `reigh-worker/source/` does. Readiness is now **heartbeat freshness + dwell**: `workers.last_heartbeat` must be within 60s of `now()` AND must have been fresh for at least 2 consecutive poll cycles (≥20s dwell). This works for BOTH Variant Fresh (custom launch — the heartbeat RPC is reachable via the injected service-role key) and Variant Update (any launch family). No metadata patching required.
2. **Variant Update `--spawn-takeover` uses the real production path.** Instead of calling `create_pod_and_wait` alone, it calls `RunpodLifecycleMixin.spawn_worker()` from `reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/runpod/lifecycle.py`, which internally invokes `create_pod_and_wait` + `start_worker_process` (render → upload → launch `worker_startup.template.sh`). This produces a real production-shaped stock worker, which the harness then takes over.
3. **Process-family aware helpers.** `capture_current_worker_cmdline()` returns `WorkerProcessInfo(family, cmdline, pid)` where `family` is `'supervisor'` (if `run_worker.py` is detected) or `'direct'` (if `worker.py` is detected without a parent supervisor). `kill_supervisor_and_worker()` matches BOTH `run_worker.py` AND `worker.py` (and `python -m source.runtime.worker` variants) via multiple `pkill -f` patterns. On teardown, the driver relaunches via the captured family: `run_worker.py` for supervisor pods, `python worker.py` (matching the template's launch line) for direct pods.
4. **Safety gate gains `allow_fresh_heartbeat` parameter.** The in-flight PAT-work query ALWAYS runs (we never want to clobber live user work). But the fresh-heartbeat rejection is skipped when the caller owns the pod (`--spawn-takeover` case). `assert_safe_to_take_over(db, pod_id, user_id, *, allow_fresh_heartbeat=False)`. B-existing passes `False`; B-fresh-takeover passes `True` because it spawned the worker itself moments ago — fresh heartbeat is expected, not a danger signal.

**Constraints:** additive only; no edits to orchestrator production paths or `reigh-worker/source/`; no force-push; no push to `main`; test token loaded only from env `REIGH_LIVE_TEST_TOKEN`.

---

## Phase 1: Prep / Finalize Unknowns (Read-Only)

### Step 1: Verify token schema + user-queue state (`reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/stage1_probe.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** a one-shot probe that (a) asserts `user_api_tokens` has plain `token` column (no `jti_hash`), (b) resolves `REIGH_LIVE_TEST_TOKEN` to a single `user_id`, (c) dumps any `tasks` rows for that user with `status in ('Queued','In Progress')` and `coalesce(params->>'live_test','false') <> 'true'`. Writes `scripts/live_test/stage1_findings.md` (gitignored).
2. **Additionally confirm** by grepping `reigh-worker/source/` that no code writes `workers.metadata.startup_phase`. Record the finding — this locks in the v3 decision to drop the predicate from the readiness waiter.

### Step 2: Pin orchestrator spawn + template path (`reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/runpod/lifecycle.py`, `runpod/client.py`, `worker_startup.template.sh`, `runpod/api.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Read** `lifecycle.py` for `RunpodLifecycleMixin.spawn_worker` and `start_worker_process` — confirm their signatures and how they sequence `create_pod_and_wait` + template render/upload/launch. These are the functions Variant Update `--spawn-takeover` will call directly.
2. **Read** `runpod/client.py` for `RUNPOD_GPU_TYPE`, `RUNPOD_WORKER_IMAGE`, `RUNPOD_TEMPLATE_ID`, storage volume + RAM tier constants — mirror these in `scripts/live_test/config.py`.
3. **Read** `worker_startup.template.sh` around lines 167-183 and 355-366 for the apt deps + uv install sequence AND to confirm the exact shell shape of the launched process (`python worker.py ...` directly, no supervisor). Record this as the "direct" process family signature for the kill/capture helpers.
4. **Read** `reigh-app/src/shared/components/SettingsModal/commandUtils.ts` to pin the `python run_worker.py` arg order for Variant Fresh.

### Step 3: Resolve models + pin fixture payloads
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Grep** `reigh-worker/source/` for `ltx` / `LTX` (especially `task_handlers/tasks/travel_guidance.py` and any model registry) to pick an actual supported LTX model id.
2. **Inventory** fixtures under `reigh-worker/artifacts/worker-matrix/`:
   - `20260316T135006000511Z/qwen_image_basic/captured_generation_task.json` → `qwen_image_t2i`
   - `.../qwen_image_edit_basic/captured_generation_task.json` → `qwen_image_edit`
   - `.../z_image_turbo_i2i_basic/captured_generation_task.json` → `z_image_turbo_i2i`
   - `20260316T141738351426Z/qwen_image_style_db_task/captured_generation_task.json` → `qwen_image_style`
   - `20260316T140127343314Z/wan22_i2v_individual_segment/captured_generation_task.json` → `individual_travel_segment`
3. **Consult** `reigh-worker/scripts/create_test_task.py` and `reigh-worker/scripts/preview/fixtures.py` for travel-orchestrator task templates. Clone + mutate for `travel_orchestrator_wan2_1seg` and `travel_orchestrator_ltx`.
4. **Pick** 2 high-contrast anchor images with clear subjects. Bake URLs into `config.py`; CLI-overridable.
5. **Verify** `WGP_TASK_TYPES` at `reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/task_types.py:26-46` contains `z_image_turbo_i2i`, `qwen_image_style`, `qwen_image_2512`.

**Deliverables:** `stage1_findings.md` with: schema state, resolved user_id, queue-clean assertion, `startup_phase` absence confirmation, `spawn_worker`/`start_worker_process` signatures, RunPod constants, "direct" vs "supervisor" process signatures, LTX model id, fixture path map.
**Validation:** Probe runs clean; all fixtures parse; LTX model id verified as real source string.

---

## Phase 2: Shared Primitives (`reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/`)

### Step 4: Package scaffold + config (`__init__.py`, `config.py`, `logger.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Create** package that adds `reigh-worker-orchestrator/` to `sys.path` at import time so `from gpu_orchestrator.database import DatabaseClient`, `from gpu_orchestrator.runpod.api import get_pod_ssh_details, terminate_pod`, `from gpu_orchestrator.runpod.ssh import SSHClient`, AND `from gpu_orchestrator.runpod.lifecycle import RunpodLifecycleMixin` resolve cleanly.
2. **`config.py`** loads from env: `REIGH_LIVE_TEST_TOKEN`, `RUNPOD_API_KEY`, `SUPABASE_URL`, `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`. Holds RunPod defaults from Step 2, fixture path map from Step 3, per-type timeouts (image=15m, individual_travel_segment=25m, travel_orchestrator=40m).

### Step 5: Token resolver (`token_resolver.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Implement** `resolve_token_to_user_id(db, token) -> str` via `db.supabase.table('user_api_tokens').select('user_id').eq('token', token).single().execute()`. Raise on zero/multiple rows.

### Step 6: Preflight user-queue check (`preflight.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Implement** `assert_user_queue_clean(db, user_id)` — joins `tasks` to `projects` where `projects.user_id = ?` and filters `status in ('Queued','In Progress') and coalesce(params->>'live_test','false') <> 'true'`. Raises `UnexpectedUserWorkError` listing offending task ids.
2. **Implement** `get_or_create_live_test_project(db, user_id) -> project_id` — looks up `projects` with `user_id=? and name='live-test'`; inserts if missing.

### Step 7: Task spoofer (`task_spoofer.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** `load_fixture(case_name) -> dict` reading from `config.FIXTURES`.
2. **Implement** `insert_spoof_task(db, project_id, task_type, params_overrides) -> task_id` — clones fixture, merges overrides, sets `project_id`, `status='Queued'`, stamps `params.live_test=True`, inserts via `db.supabase.table('tasks').insert(...)`.

### Step 8: Completion poller (`completion_poller.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** `poll_until_complete(db, task_id, project_id, *, timeout_sec, interval_sec=5) -> TaskResult`. Polls `tasks` for terminal status, fetches `output_location`, queries `generations` in `(task.created_at, now)` window.
2. **Returns** `TaskResult(task_id, case_name, task_type, final_status, output_location, generation_ids, elapsed_sec, error_summary)`. Never raises on task failure.

### Step 9: Heartbeat waiter — heartbeat + dwell (`heartbeat_waiter.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Implement** `wait_until_ready(db, worker_id, *, timeout_sec=900, interval_sec=10, dwell_polls=2)` that polls the `workers` table for `last_heartbeat > now() - interval '60 seconds'`. Readiness = the freshness condition has held for `dwell_polls` consecutive polls (default 2 → ~20s dwell). Does NOT check `startup_phase` or `ready_for_tasks` — neither is reliably written by worker source.
2. **Source-level comment** explaining WHY: a repo-wide grep (verified in Stage 1) shows only `worker_startup.template.sh` writes `metadata.startup_phase`, so requiring it would make Variant Fresh unachievable. Heartbeat RPCs ARE reachable because both variants inject `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY` into the pod env.

### Step 10: PAT-aware safety gate with ownership flag (`safety_gate.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Implement** `assert_safe_to_take_over(db, pod_id, user_id, *, allow_fresh_heartbeat: bool = False)`. Raises `UnsafeTakeoverError` if EITHER:
   - Any `tasks` row with `status='In Progress' AND generation_started_at > now() - interval '90 seconds'` joined through `projects.user_id = user_id` (PAT-work guard — ALWAYS runs regardless of `allow_fresh_heartbeat`, because we never want to clobber live user work).
   - `workers.last_heartbeat < 60s` AND `allow_fresh_heartbeat=False`. When `allow_fresh_heartbeat=True`, fresh heartbeat is permitted (caller owns the pod, e.g., just spawned it).
2. **Docstring** explaining why: B-existing passes `False` (fresh heartbeat on a pod we didn't spawn is a danger signal); B-fresh-takeover passes `True` (we just spawned the worker ourselves; fresh heartbeat is expected). The in-flight PAT-work check remains active in BOTH cases — it's the primary clobber guard.
3. **Unit test** both paths: (a) rejects fresh heartbeat when `allow_fresh_heartbeat=False`, (b) permits fresh heartbeat when `True` but still rejects live PAT work.

### Step 11: Launch command builders (`launch_command.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Implement** `build_run_worker_command(workdir, *, reigh_token, supabase_url, worker_id, wgp_profile, idle_release_minutes) -> str` producing:
   ```
   cd <workdir> && nohup uv run --python 3.10 --extra cuda124 \
     python run_worker.py \
       --supabase-url <url> --reigh-access-token <token> --worker <id> \
       --wgp-profile <N> --idle-release-minutes <N> --save-logging logs/worker.log \
     > logs/startup.log 2>&1 &
   ```
   Mirrors `reigh-app/src/shared/components/SettingsModal/commandUtils.ts`. Used by Variant Fresh (custom install, new dir) and by Variant Update when restoring pods whose captured family was `supervisor`.
2. **Implement** `build_direct_worker_command(workdir, *, cli_args: list[str]) -> str` that rebuilds the `python worker.py ...` invocation from a captured `cmdline` list (the template's launch shape). Used by Variant Update teardown when restoring a pod whose captured family was `direct` (production-template-launched).
3. **Unit-test** both builders: `build_run_worker_command` contains `run_worker.py` and `--idle-release-minutes 0`; `build_direct_worker_command` round-trips a sample template cmdline.

### Step 12: SSH bootstrap helpers — both process families (`ssh_bootstrap.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Thin wrapper** around `SSHClient` + `get_pod_ssh_details`:
   - `open_session(pod_id, api_key) -> SSHClient`.
   - `clone_repo_into(ssh, workdir, repo_url, branch)`.
   - `run_install(ssh, workdir)` — apt deps from template.sh + `uv sync --locked --extra cuda124`.
   - `export_env(env: dict) -> str` — builds `export K=V` preamble. MUST include `REIGH_ACCESS_TOKEN`, `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`, `SUPABASE_URL`, `WORKER_DB_CLIENT_AUTH_MODE=worker`.
   - **`capture_current_worker_cmdline(ssh) -> WorkerProcessInfo | None`** — runs `ps -eo pid,args | grep -E '(run_worker\\.py|worker\\.py|source\\.runtime\\.worker)' | grep -v grep` and parses output. Returns `WorkerProcessInfo(family='supervisor' | 'direct', cmdline=list[str], pid=int)`:
     - `family='supervisor'` if `run_worker.py` is in the cmdline.
     - `family='direct'` if `worker.py` (without a `run_worker.py` parent) is in the cmdline — matches template-launched pods.
     - Returns None if no worker process is running.
   - **`kill_supervisor_and_worker(ssh)`** — runs `pkill -f run_worker.py; pkill -f 'python worker.py'; pkill -f 'source.runtime.worker'; sleep 2` and verifies via `pgrep` loop (up to 10s). Matches BOTH families; safe if one pattern doesn't match.
   - `launch_worker_detached(ssh, command_line)` — runs a pre-built command string.
   - `fetch_worker_logs(ssh, workdir, lines=300)` — `tail -n <lines> logs/startup.log logs/worker.log`.

### Step 13: Terminate guard (`terminate_guard.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Implement** `guarded_terminate(pod_id, api_key, *, no_terminate: bool)` — skips `terminate_pod` if `no_terminate` or env `REIGH_LIVE_TEST_NO_TERMINATE=1`. Wrapped in driver try/finally.

### Step 14: Matrix runner + report (`matrix.py`, `report.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Define** `MATRIX` as 8 `MatrixCase(name, task_type, fixture_key, param_overrides, timeout_sec)`:
   - `travel_orchestrator_wan2_1seg` (40m), `travel_orchestrator_ltx` (40m, LTX id from Step 3).
   - `individual_travel_segment` (25m, fixture `wan22_i2v_individual_segment`).
   - `qwen_image_style` (15m, fixture `qwen_image_style_db_task`, style ref = anchor A).
   - `qwen_image_t2i` (15m, fixture `qwen_image_basic`).
   - `qwen_image_2512` (15m, mutation of `qwen_image_basic`).
   - `qwen_image_edit` (15m, fixture `qwen_image_edit_basic`, input = anchor B).
   - `z_image_turbo_i2i` (15m, fixture `z_image_turbo_i2i_basic`, `_i2i` suffix — verified in Stage 1).
2. **Implement** `run_matrix(db, project_id, cases) -> list[TaskResult]` — loops cases, spoofs + polls each, CONTINUES on individual failures.
3. **Implement** `write_report(results, variant, pod_id, out_dir)` — `report.json` + `report.md`, consolidated `N/8 passed`.

### Step 15: Unit tests (`tests/test_primitives.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Pytest** with mocked `DatabaseClient` / `SSHClient`:
   - `test_token_resolver_returns_user_id` / `_raises_on_missing`.
   - `test_preflight_raises_on_stray_user_work` / `_passes_when_clean`.
   - `test_task_spoofer_stamps_live_test_and_queued_status`.
   - `test_completion_poller_returns_on_complete` / `_times_out` / `_records_failure`.
   - `test_heartbeat_waiter_requires_dwell_not_startup_phase` — mock sends fresh heartbeat, waiter accepts after `dwell_polls` cycles without requiring `startup_phase`.
   - `test_safety_gate_rejects_fresh_in_progress_for_user` (always runs).
   - `test_safety_gate_rejects_fresh_heartbeat_when_not_allowed`.
   - `test_safety_gate_permits_fresh_heartbeat_when_allowed_but_still_rejects_live_pat_work`.
   - `test_terminate_guard_respects_env_var` / `_respects_cli_flag` / `_skips_on_exception_path`.
   - `test_build_run_worker_command_uses_run_worker_py_and_idle_zero`.
   - `test_build_direct_worker_command_roundtrips_template_cmdline`.
   - `test_capture_cmdline_detects_supervisor_family_from_run_worker_ps`.
   - `test_capture_cmdline_detects_direct_family_from_template_ps`.
   - `test_kill_supervisor_and_worker_patterns_cover_both_families` — verifies `pkill` command strings mention both `run_worker.py` and `worker.py`.

**Deliverables:** ~13 primitive modules + comprehensive unit tests.
**Validation:** `uv run pytest reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/tests/` green.

---

## Phase 3: Variant Fresh Driver

### Step 16: Driver (`variant_fresh.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Flow** (called from `main.py --variant fresh`):
   - Resolve token → user_id; `assert_user_queue_clean`; `get_or_create_live_test_project`.
   - `pod = create_pod_and_wait(api_key, gpu_type_id=RUNPOD_GPU_TYPE, image_name=RUNPOD_WORKER_IMAGE, template_id=RUNPOD_TEMPLATE_ID, env_vars={'REIGH_ACCESS_TOKEN': token, 'SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY': service_key, 'SUPABASE_URL': supabase_url, 'WORKER_DB_CLIENT_AUTH_MODE': 'worker'}, name='reigh-live-test-fresh-<ts>', ...)`.
   - `ssh = open_session(pod_id, api_key)`.
   - `clone_repo_into(ssh, '/workspace/Reigh-Worker-LiveTest', 'https://github.com/banodoco/Reigh-Worker.git', branch=args.ref or 'main')`.
   - `run_install(ssh, '/workspace/Reigh-Worker-LiveTest')`.
   - `cmd = build_run_worker_command('/workspace/Reigh-Worker-LiveTest', reigh_token=token, ..., wgp_profile=3, idle_release_minutes=0)`.
   - `launch_worker_detached(ssh, export_env(env) + ' && ' + cmd)`.
   - `wait_until_ready(db, worker_id=pod_id, timeout_sec=900)` — heartbeat + dwell only.
   - `results = run_matrix(db, project_id, MATRIX)`; `write_report(...)`.
2. **Finally block** fetches worker/startup log tails via `fetch_worker_logs`, then `guarded_terminate(pod_id, api_key, no_terminate=args.no_terminate)` (default: terminate).

### Step 17: Dry-run for Variant Fresh
**Scope:** Small
1. **`--dry-run`** resolves token, runs preflight, loads all fixtures, prints the full planned launch command + task list, exits 0 without touching RunPod.

---

## Phase 4: Variant Update Driver

### Step 18: Git ops with branch + worktree capture (`git_ops.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** `snapshot_local_state(submodule_path='reigh-worker') -> LocalSnapshot`:
   - `original_branch = git symbolic-ref --short HEAD` (None if detached).
   - `original_sha = git rev-parse HEAD`.
   - `is_dirty = bool(git status --porcelain)`.
   - If dirty: `git stash push --include-untracked -m "live-test-<ts>"`, record stash ref.
2. **Implement** `push_working_copy_to_temp_branch(submodule_path, snapshot) -> (branch, sha)`:
   - If dirty: checkout new branch `live-test/<ts>-<shortsha>`, `git stash apply`, `git add -A`, `git commit -m "live-test: <ts>"`.
   - If clean: checkout new branch from HEAD.
   - `git push -u origin <branch>` (never force, never touches main).
3. **Implement** `restore_local_state` + `cleanup_temp_branch` — reverse order: checkout original_branch, reset to original_sha, stash pop if present; delete temp remote+local branch if `preserve=False`.

### Step 19: Variant Update driver (`variant_update.py`)
**Scope:** Large
1. **CLI modes** (via `main.py --variant update`):
   - `--pod-id <id>`: **B-existing** — takeover an existing pod (any launch family).
   - `--spawn-takeover`: **B-fresh-takeover** — spawn a fresh production-shaped worker via `RunpodLifecycleMixin.spawn_worker()`, wait for it to be ready, then take it over.
2. **B-fresh-takeover spawn (the corrected path):**
   - Instantiate a minimal object with the `RunpodLifecycleMixin` (or call its `spawn_worker` helper directly with a stub DB adapter) — pass through the same GPU config from `config.py`. This runs the REAL production sequence: `create_pod_and_wait` → `start_worker_process` (renders `worker_startup.template.sh`, uploads it via SSH, launches `python worker.py ...`).
   - If `spawn_worker` requires a live `DatabaseClient` for `workers` table registration, pass the shared `db` instance. If it resists being called outside the orchestrator's normal lifecycle, fall back to a two-step replication: `create_pod_and_wait(...)` then `start_worker_process(pod_id, ...)` imported and called directly.
   - `wait_until_ready(db, pod_id)` — using the new heartbeat+dwell waiter, verifies the stock worker actually came online.
3. **Common takeover flow (both modes):**
   - `user_id = resolve_token_to_user_id(db, token)`; `assert_user_queue_clean(db, user_id)`.
   - `allow_fresh = (args.mode == 'spawn-takeover')`.
   - `assert_safe_to_take_over(db, pod_id, user_id, allow_fresh_heartbeat=allow_fresh)`. B-existing blocks on fresh heartbeat; B-fresh-takeover allows it because we just spawned the worker ourselves. In-flight PAT-work check runs in BOTH modes.
   - `snapshot = snapshot_local_state('reigh-worker')`; `branch, sha = push_working_copy_to_temp_branch(...)`.
   - `ssh = open_session(pod_id, api_key)`.
   - **Capture pod's pre-takeover state:**
     - `prev_remote_branch = ssh exec 'cd /workspace/Reigh-Worker && git symbolic-ref --short HEAD 2>/dev/null || echo DETACHED'`.
     - `prev_remote_sha = ssh exec 'cd /workspace/Reigh-Worker && git rev-parse HEAD'`.
     - `prev_proc = capture_current_worker_cmdline(ssh)` — returns `WorkerProcessInfo` with `family` ∈ `{'supervisor','direct'}`, or None.
   - `cd /workspace/Reigh-Worker && git fetch origin && git checkout <branch> && git pull && uv sync --locked --extra cuda124`.
   - `kill_supervisor_and_worker(ssh)` — matches both families.
   - Export env (PAT + service-role + `WORKER_DB_CLIENT_AUTH_MODE=worker`).
   - `launch_worker_detached(ssh, build_run_worker_command('/workspace/Reigh-Worker', ..., idle_release_minutes=0))` — takeover always launches via `run_worker.py` (the user-approved local-worker path).
   - `wait_until_ready(db, pod_id)`; `run_matrix(...)`; `write_report(...)`.
4. **Pod cleanup (success):**
   - If `prev_remote_branch == 'DETACHED'` OR matches `live-test/*`: skip restore, log "Pre-takeover pod state was scratch; leaving worker stopped."
   - Else: `kill_supervisor_and_worker(ssh)`, `cd /workspace/Reigh-Worker && git checkout <prev_remote_branch> && git reset --hard <prev_remote_sha> && uv sync --locked --extra cuda124`.
   - **Family-aware relaunch:** if `prev_proc is None`, leave stopped and log. If `prev_proc.family == 'supervisor'`, relaunch via the captured cmdline (which invokes `run_worker.py`). If `prev_proc.family == 'direct'`, rebuild the template-style command via `build_direct_worker_command(workdir, cli_args=prev_proc.cmdline)` and launch it — matches the shape `worker_startup.template.sh` originally used.
   - `cleanup_temp_branch(..., preserve=False)`.
5. **Pod cleanup (failure):** leave the pod on the test branch, `cleanup_temp_branch(..., preserve=True)`, log branch name.
6. **Always (finally):** `restore_local_state('reigh-worker', snapshot)`. `guarded_terminate(pod_id, api_key, no_terminate=args.no_terminate)` — default `no_terminate=True` for Variant Update.

### Step 20: Single entrypoint (`main.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **CLI:** `python -m scripts.live_test.main --variant {fresh,update} [--pod-id ID | --spawn-takeover] [--no-terminate | --terminate] [--dry-run] [--ref BRANCH] [--wgp-profile N] [--timeout-image 900 --timeout-travel-segment 1500 --timeout-travel-orchestrator 2400] [--anchor-image-a URL --anchor-image-b URL] [--serial]`.
2. **Defaults:** Variant fresh → `--terminate`. Variant update → `--no-terminate`. Both honor env `REIGH_LIVE_TEST_NO_TERMINATE=1`.

---

## Phase 5: Smoke + First Real Runs

### Step 21: Smoke script (`smoke.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **No-GPU sanity:** (a) resolves token, (b) runs preflight, (c) loads all 8 fixtures + validates JSON, (d) calls `build_run_worker_command(...)` and `build_direct_worker_command(...)` and prints both, (e) optionally runs `assert_safe_to_take_over` (with `allow_fresh_heartbeat=False`) against a user-supplied `--pod-id`, (f) exits 0/1.

### Step 22: First real run — Variant Fresh
**Scope:** Small (operational)
1. **Run** `python -m scripts.live_test.main --variant fresh --no-terminate`. Iterate until 8/8 pass, then promote to terminate-on-success.

### Step 23: First real run — Variant Update
**Scope:** Small (operational)
1. **Run** `python -m scripts.live_test.main --variant update --spawn-takeover` first. Never run Variant Update against a production pod on the first attempt.

---

## Phase 6: Cleanup

### Step 24: Gitignore + hygiene
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** to `reigh-worker/.gitignore`: `scripts/live_test/runs/`, `scripts/live_test/stage1_findings.md`, `scripts/live_test/.env*`.
2. **Verify** `git -C reigh-worker ls-remote origin 'refs/heads/live-test/*'` is empty.
3. **Verify** test token not hardcoded: `grep -r '3HKcoLeJAFFfTFFeRV6Eu7Lq' reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/` returns zero.
4. **Verify** `reigh-worker/source/` and `reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/` are untouched.

---

## Execution Order

1. **Phase 1** (read-only probes, constants, confirm `startup_phase` absence, inventory `spawn_worker` + `start_worker_process` signatures) before any code.
2. **Phase 2** primitives + comprehensive unit tests — covers both process families and both safety-gate modes before any GPU minutes.
3. **Phase 3** Variant Fresh driver.
4. **Phase 4** Variant Update driver (builds on fresh helpers + adds `spawn_worker` integration).
5. **Phase 5** smoke → Variant Fresh real run → Variant Update `--spawn-takeover` real run.
6. **Phase 6** cleanup after green runs.

## Validation Order

1. **Unit tests** (`uv run pytest reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/tests/`) — seconds, no network. Includes dual-family process detection, dwell-based readiness, dual safety-gate modes.
2. **Stage 1 probe** — verifies token, schema, clean queue, `startup_phase` absence in worker source.
3. **Smoke** — no GPU, prints both launch-command shapes.
4. **Variant Fresh dry-run** — prints full plan.
5. **First real Variant Fresh run** with `--no-terminate`, iterate until 8/8 pass.
6. **Promote** Variant Fresh to terminate-on-success.
7. **Variant Update `--spawn-takeover`** against a freshly spawned test pod, iterate until 8/8 pass.
8. **Existing orchestrator tests** (`uv run pytest reigh-worker-orchestrator/tests/`) still green.
9. **Existing worker tests** still green.

When `True`, fresh heartbeat is permitted (B-fresh-takeover, because caller owns the pod). The in-flight PAT-work check runs unchanged in both cases. Both branches unit-tested",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Variant Update `--spawn-takeover` calls `RunpodLifecycleMixin.spawn_worker()` from `reigh-worker-orchestrator/gpu_orchestrator/runpod/lifecycle.py` (or equivalently calls `create_pod_and_wait` + `start_worker_process` as a two-step sequence), so the stock production startup template actually runs on the spawned pod",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Variant Update `--spawn-takeover` passes `allow_fresh_heartbeat=True` to the safety gate; `--pod-id` passes `False` \u2014 verifiable in `variant_update.py`",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "`capture_current_worker_cmdline` returns a `WorkerProcessInfo(family, cmdline, pid)` where `family` is `'supervisor'` if `run_worker.py` is present in the ps output, or `'direct'` if `worker.py` is present without a `run_worker.py` parent. Returns None when no worker is running. Both family detections are unit-tested with sample ps output",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "`kill_supervisor_and_worker` runs `pkill -f` against BOTH `run_worker.py` AND `python worker.py` patterns (plus `source.runtime.worker`), verified by `pgrep` loop. Unit test asserts both patterns are in the executed command string",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Variant Update teardown relaunches the pod's original worker using family-aware logic: `supervisor` family \u2192 relaunch via captured cmdline (invokes `run_worker.py`); `direct` family \u2192 relaunch via `build_direct_worker_command`. If `prev_proc` is None, leave stopped and log",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Terminate guard skips `terminate_pod` when `REIGH_LIVE_TEST_NO_TERMINATE=1` OR `--no-terminate` is passed, on both normal exit and exception paths \u2014 covered by unit tests",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Variant Fresh clones into `/workspace/Reigh-Worker-LiveTest` (NOT `/workspace/Reigh-Worker`)",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Variant Update git ops snapshot original branch name via `git symbolic-ref --short HEAD`, stash uncommitted work with `git stash push --include-untracked`, and restore both on teardown",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Variant Update git ops push to temp branch `live-test/<ts>-<sha>` only \u2014 never to `main`, never force-push, never `--no-verify`",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Variant Update on success deletes the temp remote branch, restores the pod's original remote branch via captured `prev_remote_branch`/`prev_remote_sha`, and relaunches the family-appropriate worker; on failure preserves the branch and logs its name prominently",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {
      "criterion": "Variant Update skips pod restore when pre-takeover branch was detached or matched `live-test/*`, logs the skip, and leaves the worker stopped",
      "priority": "must"
    },
    {

    "id": "issue_hints-1",
    "concern": "Did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: Checked the approved `B-fresh-takeover` flow against Step 19. A just-spawned stock worker necessarily has a fresh heartbeat, so the fresh-takeover mode blocks itself instead of progressing.",
    "status": "addressed",
    "severity": "significant"
  },

      "id": "DEBT-007",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",

      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",

      ]

    {
      "id": "DEBT-106",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-107",
      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "did-the-work-fully-address-the-issue-hints-user-notes-and-approved-plan-requirements": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-013",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the package is internally inconsistent. the plan body says to keep `activepairdata` in `usestate` and make phase 2 optional, but the attached metadata and success criteria still describe the previous derive-via-`usememo` / remove-`setactivepairdata` / simplify-`onpairclick` plan. that means the approved-plan requirements are only partially coherent as presented.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-108",
      "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the linux distro note is documentation-only; the generated install command in step 7 still runs `apt-get install python3.10-venv python3.10-dev ffmpeg` without any pre-check that the package exists. users on ubuntu 24.04+ who ignore the doc and try to copy-paste the command will hit a confusing `e: unable to locate package python3.10-venv` from apt rather than a targeted error from commandutils.ts. a one-line detection pre-check (e.g., `apt-cache show python3.10-venv >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo 'install deadsnakes ppa first \u2014 see readme'; exit 1; }`) would convert the silent failure into an actionable error, but the plan does not add this.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }
  ],
  "does-the-change-touch-all-locations-and-supporting-infrastructure": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-014",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: phase 1 still misses a real supporting-data location: `trailingpairdata` lives outside `pairdatabyindex` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotpresentationadapter.ts#l111), but the proposed sync logic only considers `pairdatabyindex`. if trailing slot regenerate is part of the bug surface, that location is not covered.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-110",
      "concern": "does the change touch all locations and supporting infrastructure?: the non-swallowing helper `update_worker_phase_strict` is a new shell function, but phase 1 step 2 introduces it only as pseudo-code. there is no concrete note that the helper must handle supabase http non-2xx responses as failures (curl returns 0 even on http 4xx/5xx by default unless `-f` or `--fail-with-body` is used). if the implementer ports `update_worker_phase` verbatim with only `|| return 0` removed, a 500 from supabase would still report success because curl's exit code is 0. the plan should specify `curl --fail` or explicit http status code checking in the strict variant.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }
  ],

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-109",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
      ]
    }
  ],

  {
    "subsystem": "are-the-proposed-changes-technically-correct",
    "total_occurrences": 5,
    "plan_count": 2,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-007",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: for ordinary pairs, the new diagnosis is not well-supported by the repo. the current sync effect in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l62) already syncs `url` and `primaryvariantid`, and the regenerate path reads exactly `starturl`, `endurl`, `startgenid`, `endgenid`, `startvariantid`, and `endvariantid` from `segmentslotmode.pairdata` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/domains/media-lightbox/hooks/usevideoregeneratemode.ts#l438). adding `thumburl` and `generationid` to the sync does not, by itself, explain why an old image url is still being used for regular pairs.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-008",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the fix is incomplete for extra trailing slots. `handlepairclick` can populate `activepairdata` from `trailingpairdata` when `pairindex === pairdatabyindex.size` in [/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/travel-between-images/components/shotimageseditor/hooks/usesegmentslotmode.ts#l121), but the proposed sync effect only reads `pairdatabyindex.get(segmentslotlightboxindex)`. for those trailing-slot cases `fresh` is `undefined`, so no image refresh happens.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-018",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the merge pseudocode does not actually sync 'all image fields' as claimed. it omits `id` and `position` from change detection, and when `fresh.startimage` or `fresh.endimage` becomes `null` it preserves the stale previous object instead of syncing that null state. as written, it also needs explicit null-guards around spreads of `prev.startimage` / `prev.endimage` to be safe in strict typescript.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-106",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the plan's claim that `worker_state.py:200-204` (the 30-minute startup safety net) catches abandoned pods that exited before patching is slightly imprecise. line 200-204 only runs when `in_startup_phase` is true, which requires `startup_phase` to be in `('deps_installing', 'deps_verified', 'worker_starting')` per line 161. a pod that exits before its first patch has `startup_phase = none`, so `in_startup_phase` is false and the code falls through to the normal timeout checks (active_stale / not_claiming) at line 207+, not the 30-minute startup cap. both paths eventually reap the worker, so the end result is the same, but the overview's reasoning (naming 200-204 specifically) is wrong. worth correcting so future readers don't mis-reason about the invariant.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-107",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked worker_state.py line 161 (`in_startup_phase = startup_phase in (...)`) against the plan's assumption that subsequent lenient writes are safe. if deps_installing succeeds but deps_verified silently fails, startup_phase stays at 'deps_installing' and the 30-minute cap from line 200-204 applies \u2014 even though the pod is actually progressing through `uv sync`. on a slow runpod image with cold wheel downloads, `uv sync --extra cuda124` plus wan2gp deps can take 20+ minutes. there's no concrete benchmark in the plan that says the 30-minute cap is safely above the expected sync time; if `uv sync` legitimately takes 25 minutes and the deps_verified patch fails on a transient network blip, the orchestrator will terminate a healthy pod. plan should either bump the cap or note that deps_verified swallowing is acceptable because the cap is generously above typical sync time.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      }
    ]
  },

- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.
- Every check must end with at least one finding. Never leave a `findings` array empty. If you found no issue, add one detailed `flagged: false` finding explaining what you checked and why it appears clear.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
Bad: {"detail": "No issue found", "flagged": false}  ← too brief, will be rejected
Bad: {"detail": "The hints suggest approach X but the plan uses Y. However Y is consistent with X's intent.", "flagged": false}  ← a different approach than the hints IS a flag. You found a divergence — flag it. The gate decides if it's acceptable.

After filling in checks, add any additional concerns to the `flags` array (e.g., security, performance, dependencies).
Use the standard format (id, concern, category, severity_hint, evidence). This array can be empty.

Workflow: read the file → investigate → read file again → add finding → write file back. Repeat for each check.

The review lands in two buckets. I marked `FLAG-006`, `FLAG-008`, and `FLAG-009` as verified resolved: the v3 plan did fix the `startup_phase` dependency, the dual launch-family handling, and the fresh-heartbeat self-block in `B-fresh-takeover`.

I re-opened `FLAG-007` and added `FLAG-010`. The remaining substantive problem is that the plan still overstates `RunpodLifecycleMixin.spawn_worker()` as if it also launches the production startup template, and it still conflates `runpod_id` with `worker_id` in the fresh-takeover path. In the actual repo, callers create a separate worker record, call `spawn_worker(worker_id)`, then separately call `start_worker_process(pod_id, worker_id, ...)`. If execution follows the current plan literally, readiness polling and takeover relaunch will target the wrong identifier.
